🧭 Manipulation & Dexterous

· 发展脉络· Development

该方向聚焦于具身智能中的灵巧操作与多模态交互，核心挑战在于解决从感知到执行的长视距规划、接触力控制的精确性以及跨机器人形态的泛化能力。发展脉络从早期的模仿学习与扩散策略起步，迅速演进至融合视觉、语言与触觉的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）大模型，并引入世界模型进行长程推理与仿真。近期趋势强调利用生成式视频、触觉反馈及物理一致性约束，实现从仿真到现实的无缝迁移及复杂灵巧手的自主操作。This direction focuses on dexterous manipulation and multimodal interaction in embodied intelligence. The core challenges lie in solving long-horizon planning from perception to execution, precision in contact force control, and generalization across robot morphologies. The development trajectory began with early imitation learning and diffusion policies, rapidly evolving into vision-language-action (VLA) large models that integrate vision, language, and touch, while incorporating world models for long-horizon reasoning and simulation. Recent trends emphasize leveraging generative video, tactile feedback, and physical consistency constraints to achieve seamless sim-to-real transfer and autonomous manipulation with complex dexterous hands.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 低成本全身遥操作与数据采集Low-cost whole-body teleoperation and data collection

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

低成本全身遥操作与数据采集Low-cost whole-body teleoperation and data collection

本方向核心在于打破昂贵动捕与专用遥操作硬件的垄断，利用VR、单目相机、手持设备及触觉手套等低成本方案，实现人形或双臂机器人的全身遥操作与高效数据采集。演进脉络从早期的硬件简化（如Mobile ALOHA）转向多模态融合与数据增强（如DexCap、EgoDex），进而探索仿真合成数据（VLK）与互联网视频迁移（Do as I Do）以解决长尾场景。最新趋势聚焦于解决遥操作中的接触力控、空间泛化及人机交互语义对齐，通过混合数据策略与自适应触觉反馈，试图在低成本硬件约束下逼近人类水平的灵巧操作与全身协调。The core of this direction lies in breaking the monopoly of expensive motion capture and dedicated teleoperation hardware. By utilizing low-cost solutions such as VR, monocular cameras, handheld devices, and haptic gloves, it achieves whole-body teleoperation and efficient data collection for humanoid or dual-arm robots. The evolution has shifted from early hardware simplification (e.g., Mobile ALOHA) to multi-modal fusion and data augmentation (e.g., DexCap, EgoDex), and further explores synthetic simulation data (VLK) and internet video transfer (Do as I Do) to address long-tail scenarios. The latest trends focus on solving contact force control, spatial generalization, and human-robot interaction semantic alignment in teleoperation. Through mixed data strategies and adaptive haptic feedback, it attempts to approach human-level dexterous manipulation and whole-body coordination under low-cost hardware constraints.

论文
动机: Motivation: 利用低成本柔性传感器与自适应算法，实现精细触觉反馈的数字化传输与交互。Low-cost flexible sensors and adaptive algorithms are utilized to achieve digital transmission and interaction of fine tactile feedback.
方法: Method: 通过数字刺绣技术将传感器织入手套，利用自适应算法根据用户反馈微调震动参数。Sensors are woven into gloves via digital embroidery technology, and adaptive algorithms are used to fine-tune vibration parameters based on user feedback.
推进: Advance: 实现了低成本、自适应的触觉反馈传输，为遥操作中的力觉感知提供了一种新颖且可定制的硬件解决方案。Low-cost, adaptive tactile feedback transmission is achieved, providing a novel and customizable hardware solution for force perception in teleoperation.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 为突破真实机器人灵巧操作数据昂贵且稀缺的瓶颈，试图利用海量人类视频通过视觉编辑生成机器人数据。To break through the bottleneck of expensive and scarce data for real-world robotic dexterous manipulation, this work attempts to utilize massive human videos to generate robotic data through visual editing.
方法: Method: 构建HandEdit基准，利用生成式模型将人类手部视频转换为机器人手部视角，并建立统一的评测标准。The HandEdit benchmark is constructed, utilizing generative models to convert human hand videos into robotic hand perspectives, and establishing a unified evaluation standard.
推进: Advance: 填补了具身智能中“人机视觉鸿沟”的空白，为低成本数据生成提供了统一基准，尽管物理交互细节仍有差距。This fills the gap of the 'human-robot visual gap' in embodied intelligence, providing a unified benchmark for low-cost data generation, although physical interaction details still have gaps.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决全身运动操作（Loco-Manipulation）中强化学习奖励函数设计困难及数据采集成本高昂的问题。Aiming to solve the difficulties in designing reinforcement learning reward functions and the high cost of data collection in whole-body locomotion manipulation (Loco-Manipulation).
方法: Method: 利用模型预测控制（SMPC）在仿真中生成高质量演示数据，驱动强化学习算法在稀疏奖励下学习全身策略。High-quality demonstration data is generated in simulation using Stochastic Model Predictive Control (SMPC) to drive reinforcement learning algorithms to learn whole-body policies under sparse rewards.
推进: Advance: 实现了从仿真到真机的鲁棒迁移，证明了利用自动生成的轨迹数据可替代人工示教，大幅降低了全身协调技能的学习门槛。Robust sim-to-real transfer is achieved, demonstrating that automatically generated trajectory data can replace manual teleoperation, significantly lowering the learning threshold for whole-body coordination skills.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 针对灵巧操作遥操作中高维手部控制难以由人类直接完成的痛点，旨在通过解耦任务层级来降低数据采集门槛。Aiming to address the pain point that high-dimensional hand control in dexterous teleoperation is difficult for humans to perform directly, this work seeks to lower the data collection threshold by decoupling task levels.
方法: Method: 提出嵌套策略架构，利用内层策略作为“陪练”处理细粒度手指协调，人类仅通过简单开关控制宏观进度。A nested policy architecture is proposed, utilizing an inner policy as a 'sparring partner' to handle fine-grained finger coordination, while humans control the macroscopic progress via simple switches.
推进: Advance: 证明了在低成本硬件约束下，通过“宏观规划+微观执行”的解耦模式可显著提升数据收集效率与演示成功率。It is demonstrated that under low-cost hardware constraints, the decoupled mode of 'macroscopic planning + microscopic execution' can significantly improve data collection efficiency and demonstration success rates.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 解决传统遥操作在动态环境中因局部极小值导致的机械臂停滞问题，提升低成本硬件下的实时安全性。This solves the problem of robotic arm stagnation caused by local minima in traditional teleoperation within dynamic environments, enhancing real-time safety under low-cost hardware.
方法: Method: 引入预测相对速度转向机制，用旋转速度矢量替代传统斥力势场，并针对延迟进行补偿优化。A predicted relative velocity steering mechanism is introduced, replacing traditional repulsive potential fields with rotation velocity vectors, and incorporating compensation optimization for latency.
推进: Advance: 实现了轻量级即插即用的安全补丁，有效避免了狭窄空间内的死锁，提升了遥操作在动态场景下的流畅度。A lightweight, plug-and-play safety patch is implemented, effectively avoiding deadlocks in narrow spaces and improving the fluidity of teleoperation in dynamic scenarios.
2024-01
Mobile ALOHA: Learning Bimanual Mobile Manipulation with Low-Cost Whole-Body Teleoperation
动机: Motivation: 解决移动操作机器人成本高昂且难以普及的问题，旨在让普通实验室也能负担得起全身遥操作平台。To address the high cost and difficulty in popularizing mobile manipulation robots, aiming to make whole-body teleoperation platforms affordable for ordinary laboratories.
方法: Method: 在ALOH A基础上加装轮式底盘，采用“人拉车”的全身遥操作模式，并引入静态数据共训练策略。Adding a wheeled chassis to ALOHA, adopting a 'human-pulling-car' whole-body teleoperation mode, and introducing a static data co-training strategy.
推进: Advance: 将移动操作机器人成本降至3.2万美元，证明了低成本硬件配合数据复用即可实现有效的移动操作策略学习。Reducing the cost of mobile manipulation robots to $32,000, proving that low-cost hardware combined with data reuse can achieve effective mobile manipulation policy learning.
2024-02
Universal Manipulation Interface: In-The-Wild Robot Teaching Without In-The-Wild Robots
动机: Motivation: 消除野外数据采集对昂贵机器人硬件的依赖，实现无需真机的“野外教学”。Eliminating the dependence on expensive robot hardware for field data collection, enabling 'field teaching' without real robots.
方法: Method: 利用手持夹爪配合GoPro鱼眼镜头，通过相对轨迹消除坐标依赖，实现单目深度估计与动作捕捉。Using handheld grippers combined with GoPro fisheye lenses, eliminating coordinate dependence through relative trajectories to achieve monocular depth estimation and motion capture.
推进: Advance: 在无需真机参与的情况下实现了70%的野外泛化成功率，显著降低了数据采集的门槛与成本。Achieving a 70% field generalization success rate without real robot participation, significantly lowering the threshold and cost of data collection.
2024-03
DexCap: Scalable and Portable Mocap Data Collection System for Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决灵巧手数据采集成本高、易受遮挡且难以便携的问题，追求可规模化的野外数据获取。Addressing the high cost, susceptibility to occlusion, and portability issues of dexterous hand data collection, pursuing scalable field data acquisition.
方法: Method: 开发便携式DexCap动捕系统，结合DexIL扩散策略与点云处理，实现人手动作到机械手的迁移。Developing a portable DexCap motion capture system, combining DexIL diffusion policies and point cloud processing to achieve the transfer of human hand actions to robotic hands.
推进: Advance: 实现了比遥操作快三倍的数据采集速度，且具备抗遮挡能力，为灵巧操作提供了低成本数据闭环方案。Achieving data collection speeds three times faster than teleoperation, with occlusion resistance, providing a low-cost data closed-loop solution for dexterous manipulation.
2024-03
Learning Human-to-Humanoid Real-Time Whole-Body Teleoperation
动机: Motivation: 利用纯视觉替代昂贵的动捕系统，实现人形机器人的实时全身遥操作控制。Replacing expensive motion capture systems with pure vision to achieve real-time whole-body teleoperation control for humanoid robots.
方法: Method: 提出H2O框架，通过RGB摄像头实时估计人体姿态，并利用仿真特权信息进行数据清洗与动作筛选。Proposing the H2O framework, which estimates human pose in real-time using RGB cameras and utilizes simulation privileges for data cleaning and action selection.
推进: Advance: 成功在Unitree H1上实现无需动捕服的全身实时控制，验证了纯视觉输入在人形机器人遥操作中的可行性。Successfully achieving whole-body real-time control on Unitree H1 without motion capture suits, validating the feasibility of pure vision input in humanoid robot teleoperation.
2024-06
OmniH2O: Universal and Dexterous Human-to-Humanoid Whole-Body Teleoperation and Learning
动机: Motivation: 整合VR与多模态大模型，构建通用的人形机器人全身遥操作与语言控制接口。Integrating VR and multi-modal large models to build a universal whole-body teleoperation and language control interface for humanoid robots.
方法: Method: 利用VR设备与GPT-4o结合，通过稀疏输入实现全身协调控制，并发布相关数据集。Combining VR devices with GPT-4o to achieve whole-body coordinated control through sparse input, and releasing the relevant dataset.
推进: Advance: 实现了语言指令驱动的全身协调操作，证明了稀疏视觉输入结合大模型在复杂人形控制中的潜力。Achieving whole-body coordinated manipulation driven by language instructions, demonstrating the potential of sparse visual input combined with large models in complex humanoid control.
2024-06
HumanPlus: Humanoid Shadowing and Imitation from Humans
动机: Motivation: 构建低成本闭环系统，利用单目RGB替代动捕，实现人形机器人的全身遥操作与模仿学习。Building a low-cost closed-loop system, using monocular RGB to replace motion capture, achieving whole-body teleoperation and imitation learning for humanoid robots.
方法: Method: 开发HumanPlus系统，通过单目视觉与Retargeting技术，将人类动作映射到33自由度人形机器人。Developing the HumanPlus system, using monocular vision and Retargeting technology to map human actions to a 33-degree-of-freedom humanoid robot.
推进: Advance: 在低成本硬件下实现了60-100%的短视距任务成功率，验证了单目视觉在全身遥操作中的工程落地性。Achieving a 60-100% success rate for short-range tasks under low-cost hardware, validating the engineering feasibility of monocular vision in whole-body teleoperation.
2024-07
Bunny-VisionPro: Real-Time Bimanual Dexterous Teleoperation for Imitation Learning
动机: Motivation: 利用消费级VR设备（Vision Pro）结合低成本触觉反馈，实现实时双灵巧手遥操作。Using consumer-grade VR devices (Vision Pro) combined with low-cost haptic feedback to achieve real-time dual-dexterous-hand teleoperation.
方法: Method: 结合Apple Vision Pro的高精度追踪与低成本触觉电机，构建双灵巧手遥操作与模仿学习系统。Combining the high-precision tracking of Apple Vision Pro with low-cost haptic motors to build a dual-dexterous-hand teleoperation and imitation learning system.
推进: Advance: 实现了低延迟的双手实时遥操作，证明了消费级VR设备在灵巧操作数据采集中的高效性与可行性。Achieving low-latency real-time dual-hand teleoperation, proving the efficiency and feasibility of consumer-grade VR devices in dexterous manipulation data collection.
2024-07
Open-TeleVision: Teleoperation with Immersive Active Visual Feedback
动机: Motivation: 通过沉浸式视觉反馈解决远程遥操作中的遮挡与空间感知问题，提升数据采集质量。Solving occlusion and spatial perception issues in remote teleoperation through immersive visual feedback, improving data collection quality.
方法: Method: 结合VR头显与机器人主动头部运动（Active Head），利用立体视频流与DinoV2视觉骨干网络。Combining VR headsets with robot active head motion (Active Head), utilizing stereoscopic video streams and the DinoV2 visual backbone network.
推进: Advance: 实现了跨海岸线的沉浸式遥操作，显著提升了远程操作中的遮挡处理能力与数据泛化性。Achieving immersive teleoperation across coastlines, significantly improving occlusion handling capabilities and data generalization in remote operation.
2025-05
DexUMI: Using Human Hand as the Universal Manipulation Interface for Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 消除人手与机械手之间的具身差距，实现“人手即接口”的通用灵巧操作遥操作。Eliminating the embodiment gap between human hands and robotic hands, achieving 'human hand as interface' universal dexterous manipulation teleoperation.
方法: Method: 设计定制外骨骼消除空间错位，利用SAM2进行视觉修复，并尝试解决触觉传感器漂移问题。Designing custom exoskeletons to eliminate spatial misalignment, utilizing SAM2 for visual repair, and attempting to solve haptic sensor drift issues.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需复杂空间映射的灵巧操作，证明了外骨骼方案在消除具身差异方面的有效性，尽管硬件适配成本高。Achieving dexterous manipulation without complex spatial mapping, proving the effectiveness of exoskeleton solutions in eliminating embodiment differences, despite high hardware adaptation costs.
2025-05
EgoDex: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Large-Scale Egocentric Video
动机: Motivation: 利用消费级AR设备（Vision Pro）构建史上最大规模的灵巧操作数据集，填补精细手部动作标注空白。Using consumer-grade AR devices (Vision Pro) to build the largest-scale dexterous manipulation dataset to date, filling the gap in fine-grained hand action annotation.
方法: Method: 采集829小时Ego-centric视频并配准3D手骨数据，利用ARKit进行自动化标注与轨迹预测。Collecting 829 hours of Ego-centric video and registering 3D hand bone data, utilizing ARKit for automated annotation and trajectory prediction.
推进: Advance: 提供了大规模、高精度的灵巧操作基准数据集，为VLA模型训练提供了关键的视觉-动作数据燃料。Providing a large-scale, high-precision dexterous manipulation benchmark dataset, offering critical vision-action data fuel for VLA model training.
2025-05
TWIST: Teleoperated Whole-Body Imitation System
动机: Motivation: 解决全身遥操作中运动与操作分离的问题，尝试用单一网络实现全身协调控制。Addressing the separation of motion and manipulation in whole-body teleoperation, attempting to achieve whole-body coordinated control with a single network.
方法: Method: 构建TWIST系统，采用Teacher-Student蒸馏策略，利用MoCap设备采集全身轨迹数据。Building the TWIST system, adopting a Teacher-Student distillation strategy, and using MoCap equipment to collect whole-body trajectory data.
推进: Advance: 实现了Unitree G1的全身协调操作（如边踢球边搬箱），证明了单一策略覆盖多模态任务的可能性，但依赖昂贵硬件。Achieving whole-body coordinated manipulation on Unitree G1 (e.g., kicking a ball while moving a box), proving the possibility of a single policy covering multi-modal tasks, but relying on expensive hardware.
2026-06
Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments
动机: Motivation: 利用互联网人类视频数据实现人形机器人的零样本学习，解决真机演示数据稀缺问题。Utilizing internet human video data to achieve zero-shot learning for humanoid robots, solving the scarcity of real-robot demonstration data.
方法: Method: 通过逆运动学与FK-aware训练，将Ego-Exo人类视频动作映射到60自由度人形机器人。Through inverse kinematics and FK-aware training, human video actions from Ego-Exo are mapped to a 60-degree-of-freedom humanoid robot.
推进: Advance: 实现了从人类视频到真机动作的零样本迁移，验证了利用大规模人类视频数据加速人形机器人学习的可行性。Zero-shot transfer from human videos to real-robot actions is achieved, validating the feasibility of accelerating humanoid robot learning using large-scale human video data.
2026-06
RoboTacDex: A Dexterous Visual-Tactile-Action Dataset for Humanoid Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决人形机器人灵巧操作中触觉与多视角数据融合难题，构建高保真真机数据集。The challenge of fusing tactile and multi-view data in humanoid robot dexterous manipulation is addressed, and a high-fidelity real-robot dataset is constructed.
方法: Method: 采集6k条包含触觉与多视角视频的真机轨迹数据，构建RoboTacDex基准数据集。6k real-robot trajectory data containing tactile and multi-view video are collected to build the RoboTacDex benchmark dataset.
推进: Advance: 填补了人形机器人触觉-视觉-动作多模态真机数据的空白，为后续多模态策略网络训练提供了重要基准。The gap in multi-modal real-robot data for tactile-visual-action is filled, providing a crucial benchmark for subsequent multi-modal policy network training.
2026-06
Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation
动机: Motivation: 构建开源全栈行为克隆平台，降低遥操作数据采集与复现的门槛，促进社区公平发展。An open-source full-stack behavior cloning platform is built to lower the threshold for teleoperation data collection and reproduction, promoting fair community development.
方法: Method: 发布ABC-130K大规模开源数据集，对比DiT与VLA架构，并对比不同价位机器人硬件的性能。The ABC-130K large-scale open-source dataset is released, comparing DiT and VLA architectures, and evaluating the performance of robots at different price points.
推进: Advance: 证明了低成本机器人（$8000）配合大规模开源数据也能实现与昂贵机器人相近的性能，推动了遥操作数据的普惠化。It is demonstrated that low-cost robots ($8000) combined with large-scale open-source data can achieve performance comparable to expensive robots, promoting the democratization of teleoperation data.
2026-06
Bridging Handheld and Teleoperated Supervision for Contact-Rich Manipulation via State-Gated Experts
动机: Motivation: 解决低成本手持数据与高保真遥操作数据在接触任务中的分布差异，提升接触敏感任务的控制精度。The distribution discrepancy between low-cost handheld data and high-fidelity teleoperation data in contact tasks is addressed, improving control precision for contact-sensitive tasks.
方法: Method: 提出State-Gat架构，利用门控专家混合策略，根据隐空间距离动态路由手持与遥操作数据。The State-Gat architecture is proposed, utilizing a gated mixture-of-experts strategy to dynamically route handheld and teleoperation data based on latent space distance.
推进: Advance: 有效融合了低成本手持数据与高保真遥操作数据，显著提升了接触丰富任务中的策略泛化性与控制稳定性。Low-cost handheld data and high-fidelity teleoperation data are effectively fused, significantly enhancing policy generalization and control stability in contact-rich tasks.
2026-06
Do as I Do: Dexterous Manipulation Data from Everyday Human Videos
动机: Motivation: 挖掘互联网海量视频数据中的灵巧操作信息，解决真机数据采集成本高昂且场景受限的问题。Dexterous manipulation information is mined from massive internet video data to address the high cost and limited scenarios of real-robot data collection.
方法: Method: 利用单目RGB视频进行4D重建与动力学重定向，从日常视频中提取可执行的灵巧操作数据。Monocular RGB video is used for 4D reconstruction and dynamics retargeting to extract executable dexterous manipulation data from daily videos.
推进: Advance: 验证了从互联网视频迁移到机器人控制的可行性，尽管数据清洗成本高，但为开放世界操作提供了新数据源。The feasibility of transferring from internet videos to robot control is validated; despite high data cleaning costs, it provides a new data source for open-world manipulation.
2026-06
VLK: Learning Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Synthetic Interactions in Reconstructed Scenes
动机: Motivation: 利用合成数据解决人形机器人全身运动与操作（Loco-Manipulation）的数据饥渴问题。Synthetic data is utilized to address the data hunger for whole-body locomotion and manipulation (Loco-Manipulation) in humanoid robots.
方法: Method: 基于3DGS重建真实场景，在虚拟环境中生成48k条轨迹，通过人类动作重定向实现零样本迁移。Real scenes are reconstructed based on 3DGS, generating 48k trajectories in a virtual environment, achieving zero-shot transfer through human action retargeting.
推进: Advance: 成功实现了从合成数据到真机的零样本迁移，证明了合成数据在解决长视界操作数据稀缺问题上的巨大潜力。Zero-shot transfer from synthetic data to real robots is successfully achieved, demonstrating the immense potential of synthetic data in addressing the scarcity of long-horizon manipulation data.

方法: Method: 利用VR游戏手柄和UMI夹爪进行演示，通过稀疏关键点提取与SKR重定向算法，将人类动作映射为机器人关节指令。Utilizing VR game controllers and UMI grippers for demonstrations, mapping human actions to robot joint commands via sparse keypoint extraction and SKR retargeting algorithms.

方法: Method: 将动态技能抽象为“交互窗口”，利用单帧视频配合强化学习直接生成动作策略，跳过繁琐的轨迹规划。Abstracting dynamic skills into 'interaction windows,' utilizing single-frame video combined with reinforcement learning to directly generate action policies, bypassing cumbersome trajectory planning.

动机: Motivation: 解决多障碍物环境下机器人投掷任务的安全性与鲁棒性问题，避免显式状态爆炸。The safety and robustness issues of robot throwing tasks in multi-obstacle environments are addressed, avoiding explicit state explosion.
方法: Method: 设计PFR（势场编码）将障碍物信息编码为固定网格，结合SAC算法进行动态环境下的投掷控制。PFR (Potential Field Encoding) is designed to encode obstacle information into a fixed grid, combined with the SAC algorithm for throwing control in dynamic environments.
推进: Advance: 在复杂动态环境中实现了90%的投掷成功率，证明了固定网格编码在处理高维动态障碍物时的有效性。A 90% throwing success rate is achieved in complex dynamic environments, proving the effectiveness of fixed grid encoding in handling high-dimensional dynamic obstacles.

ViHaTeleop: A Low-Cost, Lightweight Visual-Haptic Teleoperation System for Dexterous Manipulation Learning
动机: Motivation: 旨在打破昂贵力反馈遥操作系统的垄断，利用$550的低成本硬件（LRA环与SLAM）替代$10k+的专用手套，解决灵巧操作中的触觉缺失痛点。Aiming to break the monopoly of expensive force-feedback teleoperation systems, it utilizes $550 low-cost hardware (LRA rings and SLAM) to replace $10k+ specialized gloves, addressing the pain point of missing tactile feedback in dexterous manipulation.
方法: Method: 构建视觉-触觉融合系统，通过轻量级触觉传感器与视觉SLAM追踪实现低成本全身遥操作，并引入用户研究验证触觉对操作成功率的影响。Constructs a vision-tactile fusion system to achieve low-cost whole-body teleoperation via lightweight tactile sensors and visual SLAM tracking, while introducing user studies to validate the impact of tactile feedback on operation success rates.
推进: Advance: 证明了在极低硬件成本下触觉反馈能显著提升仿真环境中的任务成功率，但揭示了触觉辅助可能导致操作时间延长的权衡问题，并初步验证了其在模仿学习数据收集中的可行性。Demonstrates that tactile feedback can significantly improve task success rates in simulation environments under extremely low hardware costs, but reveals a trade-off where tactile assistance may lead to extended operation times, and preliminarily validates its feasibility in collecting imitation learning data.

触觉与力控仿真及策略Tactile and force control simulation and strategies

本方向聚焦于解决触觉仿真加速、力觉信息融合及精密力控策略学习三大核心挑战。演进脉络从早期基于简化的物理模型（如FOTS）实现快速Sim2Real，转向利用扩散模型（TacDiffusion, DIPCOM）和VLA架构（ForceVLA, OmniVTLA）处理高维接触动态。近期研究致力于通过多模态对齐（语义触觉、sEMG力估计）和统一世界模型（WAM, VT-WAM）消除感知与控制的模态鸿沟，同时利用大规模数据集（HT-Bench, Deform360）和物理约束（RelAfford）提升策略在复杂非刚性环境下的泛化能力与鲁棒性。This direction focuses on addressing three core challenges: accelerating tactile simulation, fusing force information, and learning precise force control strategies. The evolution has shifted from early simplified physical models (e.g., FOTS) for rapid Sim2Real to utilizing diffusion models (TacDiffusion, DIPCOM) and VLA architectures (ForceVLA, OmniVTLA) for high-dimensional contact dynamics. Recent research aims to eliminate the modality gap between perception and control through multi-modal alignment (semantic tactile, sEMG force estimation) and unified world models (WAM, VT-WAM), while leveraging large-scale datasets (HT-Bench, Deform360) and physical constraints (RelAfford) to enhance policy generalization and robustness in complex non-rigid environments.

方法: Method: 提出Unified force-impedance control，利用虚拟能量罐（Energy Tank）强制保证系统被动性。Proposes Unified force-impedance control, utilizing a virtual Energy Tank to enforce system passivity.
推进: Advance: 无需死磕环境模型正交性即可保证接触丢失时的稳定性，为工业场景提供了高鲁棒性方案。Guarantees stability during contact loss without requiring strict orthogonality of the environment model, providing a highly robust solution for industrial scenarios.

论文
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决接触丰富任务中触觉Sim2Real的瓶颈，特别是传统刚性体仿真无法准确模拟弹性体变形的问题。Aims to solve the bottleneck of haptic Sim2Real in contact-rich tasks, specifically the inability of traditional rigid-body simulation to accurately simulate elastic deformation.

论文
动机: Motivation: 解决长 horizon 触觉力控任务中传统强化学习因单点失败导致全轨迹惩罚、样本效率低下的连坐问题。This solves the 'guilt by association' problem in long-horizon tactile force control tasks where traditional reinforcement learning suffers from full trajectory penalties and low sample efficiency due to single-point failures.
方法: Method: 提出Temporal GRPO，引入任务阶段概念将长轨迹拆解，仅在同一阶段 rollout 间进行信用分配比较。Temporal GRPO is proposed, introducing the concept of task phases to decompose long trajectories, performing credit assignment comparisons only between rollouts within the same phase.
推进: Advance: 打破了传统轨迹级惩罚的僵化机制，显著提升了复杂非刚性环境下的长序列力控任务成功率。The rigid mechanism of traditional trajectory-level penalties is broken, significantly improving the success rate of long-sequence force control tasks in complex non-rigid environments.

FOTS: A Fast Optical Tactile Simulator for Sim2Real Learning of Tactile-Motor Robot Manipulation Skills
动机: Motivation: 解决现有触觉仿真器（如TACTO）计算效率低、难以满足Sim2Real实时性需求的问题。The low computational efficiency of existing tactile simulators (e.g., TACTO) and their inability to meet the real-time requirements of Sim2Real are addressed.
方法: Method: 提出FOTS，利用MLP结合平面阴影近似替代复杂的FEM物理引擎，在CPU上实现高速触觉仿真。FOTS is proposed, utilizing MLP combined with planar shadow approximation to replace complex FEM physics engines, achieving high-speed tactile simulation on CPUs.
推进: Advance: 将仿真速度提升至28.6 fps，显著优于传统方法，证明了简化物理模型在peg-in-hole任务中的有效性。Simulation speed is increased to 28.6 fps, significantly outperforming traditional methods and proving the effectiveness of simplified physical models in peg-in-hole tasks.

动机: Motivation: 解决精密装配任务中传统运动域策略难以处理复杂接触力分布和动态变化的问题。Solving the problem where traditional motion-domain strategies struggle to handle complex contact force distributions and dynamic changes in precision assembly tasks.
方法: Method: 提出TacDiffusion，将扩散策略直接应用于力域，生成6D力矩轨迹而非传统运动轨迹。Proposing TacDiffusion, which applies diffusion strategies directly to the force domain to generate 6D torque trajectories instead of traditional motion trajectories.
推进: Advance: 在毫米级装配任务中实现了95.7%的零样本迁移成功率，证明了力域生成策略在精密操作中的优越性。Achieving a 95.7% zero-shot transfer success rate in millimeter-level assembly tasks, demonstrating the superiority of force-domain generation strategies in precision manipulation.

方法: Method: 提出DIPCOM框架，利用扩散模型从演示中学习柔顺接触策略，替代传统的VAE基方法。Proposing the DIPCOM framework, which utilizes diffusion models to learn compliant contact strategies from demonstrations, replacing traditional VAE-based methods.

2025-01
DexForce: Extracting Force-Informed Actions From Kinesthetic Demonstrations for Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决传统遥操作模仿学习中忽略力觉信息，导致精细操作（如拧螺丝）成功率极低的问题。Addressing the issue where traditional teleoperation imitation learning ignores force information, leading to extremely low success rates in fine manipulation tasks like screwing.
方法: Method: 提出DexForce，从示教数据中反推“力感知动作”，显式利用力传感器数据指导策略学习。Proposing DexForce, which infers 'force-perceptive actions' from demonstration data, explicitly utilizing force sensor data to guide policy learning.
推进: Advance: 在精细操作中实现了76%的成功率，对比纯位置学习（0%），证明了力信息是灵巧操作的关键。Achieving a 76% success rate in fine manipulation tasks, compared to 0% for pure position learning, proving that force information is critical for dexterous manipulation.

ImplicitRDP: An End-to-End Visual-Force Diffusion Policy with Structural Slow-Fast Learning
动机: Motivation: 解决视觉与力觉频率不匹配及多模态融合中模态坍塌导致策略失效的问题。Addressing the issues of frequency mismatch between vision and force modalities and modality collapse in multimodal fusion leading to policy failure.

VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity
动机: Motivation: 解决直接模拟音频波形计算昂贵且Sim2Real困难，以及纯视觉难以感知接触滑移的问题。Addressing the high computational cost of directly simulating audio waveforms, the difficulty of Sim2Real, and the inability of pure vision to perceive contact slip.
方法: Method: 提出VibeAct，利用“接触+滑动”低维物理量作为中间件解耦感知与控制。Proposing VibeAct, which utilizes low-dimensional physical quantities of 'contact + slip' as middleware to decouple perception and control.

2026-06
RelAfford6D: Relational 6D Affordance Graphs for Constraint-Driven Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决端到端策略在开放世界操作中容易“幻觉”，缺乏物理约束导致操作失败的问题。Addressing the issue where end-to-end policies easily 'hallucinate' in open-world operations, lacking physical constraints and leading to operation failures.
方法: Method: 提出RelAfford6D，利用关系6D效用图将操作任务映射到SE(3)几何流形，引入硬物理约束。Proposing RelAfford6D, which utilizes relational 6D affordance maps to map manipulation tasks to SE(3) geometric manifolds, introducing hard physical constraints.
推进: Advance: 在抽屉、微波炉等结构化任务中比VLA更可靠，证明了物理约束对对抗策略幻觉的有效性。Demonstrating greater reliability than VLA in structured tasks like drawers and microwaves, proving the effectiveness of physical constraints against policy hallucinations.
2026-06
Tactile-WAM: Touch-Aware World Action Model with Tactile Asymmetric Attention
动机: Motivation: 解决直接将触觉Token融入视觉预训练模型导致“触觉污染”和预测性能下降的问题。Addressing the problem of 'tactile pollution' and degraded prediction performance caused by directly integrating tactile tokens into visual pre-trained models.
方法: Method: 提出Tactile-WAM，利用不对称注意力（TAAM）隔离触觉与视觉预测路径，仅动作通道接收触觉。Proposing Tactile-WAM, which utilizes asymmetric attention (TAAM) to isolate tactile and visual prediction paths, allowing only the action channel to receive tactile input.
推进: Advance: 在接触类任务中成功率暴涨，证明了架构层面的“外科手术”式隔离能有效利用触觉修正动作。Achieving a surge in success rates for contact-related tasks, proving that 'surgical' isolation at the architecture level effectively utilizes tactile correction for actions.
2026-06
ForceBand: Learning Forceful Manipulation with sEMG
动机: Motivation: 解决人类示教数据中力觉信息缺失，导致机器人难以学习精细力控操作的问题。Addressing the issue of missing force information in human demonstration data, making it difficult for robots to learn fine force control operations.
方法: Method: 提出ForceBand，利用廉价sEMG手环反推指尖力，将力觉维度注入人类示范数据。Proposes ForceBand, leveraging inexpensive sEMG wristbands to infer fingertip force and inject haptic dimensions into human demonstration data.
推进: Advance: 相比纯视觉基线误差减少一半，证明了低成本sEMG信号是视觉的有力补充。Reduces error by half compared to a pure vision baseline, demonstrating that low-cost sEMG signals are a powerful complement to vision.

动机: Motivation: 解决触觉表征学习缺乏统一基准和大规模数据集，导致模型泛化能力差的问题。Addresses the lack of a unified benchmark and large-scale datasets for haptic representation learning, which leads to poor model generalization.
方法: Method: 提出HT-Bench和HandTouch模型，构建大规模全手触觉数据集并进行三阶段渐进式训练。Proposes HT-Bench and the HandTouch model, constructing a large-scale full-hand haptic dataset and performing three-stage progressive training.
推进: Advance: 提供了涵盖检索、补全、预测等多维度的评测基准，推动了触觉基础模型的标准化发展。Provides an evaluation benchmark covering multiple dimensions such as retrieval, completion, and prediction, promoting the standardized development of haptic foundation models.

OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有仿真器常将“任务完成”等同于“安全成功”，缺乏对接触过程中机械损伤、热损伤等物理后果的量化评估。Existing simulators often equate 'task completion' with 'safe success,' lacking quantitative assessment of physical consequences such as mechanical damage and thermal damage during contact processes.
方法: Method: 构建OopsieVerse基准，集成DamageSim模块量化力、热、流体伤害，并统一跨平台（OmniGibson/MuJoCo）的健康信号以区分破坏性成功与安全成功。Constructing the OopsieVerse benchmark, integrating the DamageSim module to quantify force, thermal, and fluid damage, and unifying health signals across platforms (OmniGibson/MuJoCo) to distinguish destructive success from safe success.
推进: Advance: 首次将“痛觉”引入仿真评估，迫使VLA模型在追求任务成功时必须兼顾物理安全性，揭示了单纯视觉导向策略在接触任务中的暴力缺陷。For the first time, introducing 'pain' into simulation evaluation, forcing VLA models to consider physical safety while pursuing task success, revealing the violent defects of purely vision-oriented strategies in contact tasks.

方法: Method: 提出VT-WAM，利用Flow Matching和不对称注意力机制，强制动作查询关注触觉序列。Proposes VT-WAM, utilizing Flow Matching and an asymmetric attention mechanism to force action queries to attend to haptic sequences.
推进: Advance: 在接触丰富任务中成功率提升至71.67%，证明了触觉参与世界模型构建的可行性。Increases success rate to 71.67% in contact-rich tasks, proving the feasibility of incorporating haptics into world model construction.

ContactFlow: A video action conditioning that transfers across embodiments
动机: Motivation: 针对现有触觉仿真与策略在跨形态迁移中因依赖具体几何形态而导致的泛化瓶颈，旨在构建一种仅关注接触动态而非具体手型的通用世界模型。Addressing the generalization bottleneck in cross-morphology transfer caused by existing tactile simulations and policies relying on specific geometric morphologies, it aims to construct a universal World Model focusing solely on contact dynamics rather than specific hand shapes.

推进: Advance: 首次实现了不依赖特定形态先验的零样本跨形态接触策略迁移，证明了接触轨迹作为通用表征在消除感知与控制模态鸿沟上的有效性。It achieves zero-shot cross-morphology contact policy transfer for the first time without relying on specific morphological priors, proving the effectiveness of contact trajectories as a universal representation in bridging the gap between perception and control modalities.

TacWAM: Anchor-Guided World Action Model with Mechanics-Aware Tactile Prediction
动机: Motivation: 针对现有世界模型缺乏对接触力学精细预测能力的问题，旨在解决触觉感知与动作预测之间的模态鸿沟，同时避免训练与执行阶段因信息泄露导致的不一致。Aims to address the lack of fine-grained contact mechanics prediction in existing world models, bridging the modality gap between tactile perception and action prediction while avoiding inconsistencies caused by information leakage during training and execution phases.
方法: Method: 提出TacWAM，通过锚点引导机制和力学感知触觉预测模块，构建信息隔离架构，使模型在训练时利用未来力场变化监督学习，而在执行时阻断动作分支直接获取触觉目标信息。Proposes TacWAM, which constructs an information isolation architecture through an anchor-guided mechanism and a mechanics-aware tactile prediction module, enabling the model to utilize future force field changes for supervised learning during training while blocking the action branch from directly accessing tactile target information during execution.
推进: Advance: 成功将触觉从被动感知信号转化为主动预测目标，证明了物理信息约束能显著提升世界模型在复杂接触场景下的泛化能力与鲁棒性，实现了75%的高成功率。Successfully transforms tactile signals from passive perception to active prediction targets, demonstrating that physical information constraints can significantly enhance the generalization and robustness of world models in complex contact scenarios, achieving a 75% high success rate.

动机: Motivation: VLA模型在处理接触任务时，Flow Matching的噪声调度与力信号融合方式不当，导致精细操作能力严重下降。VLA models exhibit severely degraded fine manipulation capabilities when handling contact tasks due to inappropriate noise scheduling in Flow Matching and improper fusion of force signals.
方法: Method: 通过深度分析揭示Beta调度与力信号拼接的失效机理，提出FACT方案，调整噪声调度并引入力感知模块以优化梯度分布。Through deep analysis revealing the failure mechanism of Beta scheduling and force signal concatenation, we propose the FACT scheme, adjusting noise scheduling and introducing a force perception module to optimize gradient distribution.

CAAT: Contact-Aware Attention Scaling and Tactile Masking for Data-Efficient Contact-Rich Manipulation
动机: Motivation: Transformer架构在融合多模态时缺乏对接触状态的显式感知，导致模型在接触前后盲目地依赖单一模态。Transformer architectures lack explicit perception of contact states when fusing multimodal data, causing models to blindly rely on a single modality before and after contact.
方法: Method: 提出CAAT机制，通过接触感知注意力缩放与触觉掩码，显式地在非接触阶段屏蔽触觉、接触阶段屏蔽视觉，注入模态切换先验。We propose the CAAT mechanism, using contact-aware attention scaling and tactile masking to explicitly shield tactile input during non-contact phases and visual input during contact phases, injecting modality switching priors.
推进: Advance: 以工程化手段解决了多模态对齐的盲目性问题，在数据稀缺场景下显著提升了接触任务的样本效率与策略鲁棒性。We solve the blindness of multimodal alignment through engineering means, significantly improving sample efficiency and policy robustness for contact tasks in data-scarce scenarios.

ViTaR: Visuo-Tactile Residual Adaptation for Foundation VLA Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有视觉语言动作（VLA）模型在处理精细接触操作时存在的“触觉盲”问题，旨在避免全量微调导致的灾难性遗忘并保留基座模型的语义泛化能力。Aims to address the 'tactile blindness' problem existing in current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models when handling fine contact operations, seeking to avoid catastrophic forgetting caused by full fine-tuning while preserving the semantic generalization capabilities of the base model.
方法: Method: 提出ViTaR架构，将触觉模态从生成者降级为调节器，通过在冻结的VLA基座上引入残差适应模块来修正触觉感知偏差。Proposes the ViTaR architecture, downgrading the tactile modality from a generator to a regulator, and correcting tactile perception biases by introducing residual adaptation modules on a frozen VLA base.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需重训基座即可将操作性能提升30.6%，证明了在保持预训练语义上限的前提下，触觉微调是解决接触式操作鲁棒性的可行路径。Achieves a 30.6% improvement in manipulation performance without retraining the base model, proving that tactile fine-tuning is a viable path to solving robustness in contact-based operations while maintaining the semantic upper bound of pre-training.

Vision-Based Tactile Intelligence for Robotics: Sensing, Learning, and Embodied Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决触觉领域硬件选型、算法设计与仿真环境割裂混乱的现状，试图建立一套将传感器物理特性与AI学习任务深度绑定的统一标准。To address the fragmented and chaotic status quo of hardware selection, algorithm design, and simulation environments in the tactile field, it attempts to establish a unified standard that deeply binds sensor physical characteristics with AI learning tasks.
方法: Method: 系统梳理并分类了GelSight、DIGIT等主流触觉传感器，从弹性体材质、尺寸及光学原理出发，构建了涵盖感知、学习与操作的一体化技术路线图。Systematically reviews and classifies mainstream tactile sensors such as GelSight and DIGIT, constructing an integrated technical roadmap covering perception, learning, and operation based on elastomer materials, dimensions, and optical principles.
推进: Advance: 揭示了触觉智能的核心在于理解物理接触与动态交互而非单纯图像识别，为后续研究提供了清晰的硬件选型与仿真构建指南，避免了“各说各话”的碎片化研究。Reveals that the core of tactile intelligence lies in understanding physical contact and dynamic interaction rather than simple image recognition, providing clear guidelines for subsequent hardware selection and simulation construction to avoid fragmented research where 'everyone speaks their own language'.

UniReflex: Plug-and-Play Force Control for Pretrained Generative Policies via Fast-Slow Reflex
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决预训练生成策略（如扩散策略、VLA）在接触任务中缺乏实时力控能力的问题，避免昂贵的全量重训。Aiming to address the lack of real-time force control capabilities in pre-trained generative policies (such as diffusion policies and VLA) during contact tasks, thereby avoiding expensive full-scale retraining.
方法: Method: 提出UniReflex框架，通过钩子机制将快速反射网络嵌入预训练模型的动作头隐空间，构建快慢双系统架构。Proposing the UniReflex framework, which embeds a fast reflex network into the action head latent space of pre-trained models via a hooking mechanism, constructing a fast-slow dual-system architecture.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需重训即可赋予生成策略力控能力的即插即用方案，显著降低延迟并提升25-66倍推理速度。Achieving a plug-and-play solution that endows generative policies with force control capabilities without retraining, significantly reducing latency and improving inference speed by 25-66 times.

HiTac-WAM: A Hierarchical Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有触觉世界模型常将接触、形变和滑移混为一谈，缺乏对接触物理因果关系的显式建模，导致在复杂接触场景下预测不可靠。Existing tactile world models often conflate contact, deformation, and slip, lacking explicit modeling of contact physical causality, which leads to unreliable predictions in complex contact scenarios.
方法: Method: 提出HiTac-WAM，构建分层触觉世界动作模型，强制将接触、形变和滑移按物理因果逻辑排序，并引入停止梯度机制以解耦层级依赖。We propose HiTac-WAM, constructing a hierarchical tactile world action model that enforces a physical causal ordering of contact, deformation, and slip, and introduces a stop-gradient mechanism to decouple hierarchical dependencies.
推进: Advance: 成功将触觉预测从被动的事后分析转变为主动的事前预警，显著提升了滑移检测的排名参考能力，但绝对预测精度仍有提升空间。Successfully transforming tactile prediction from passive post-hoc analysis to proactive pre-emptive warning, significantly enhancing the ranking reference capability for slip detection, though absolute prediction accuracy still has room for improvement.

VLA大模型架构与空间智能VLA Large Model Architecture and Spatial Intelligence

本角度聚焦于VLA大模型在灵巧操作中的架构革新，旨在解决传统模型在空间理解、动作精度及推理效率上的瓶颈。演进路径从单纯堆砌数据规模（如GR-2），转向架构解耦与空间增强：CogACT通过认知与行动分离提升控制精度；TraceVLA引入视觉轨迹提示弥补时空记忆缺失；SpatialVLA强化3D几何感知以克服平面视觉局限；TinyVLA与FAST则分别从轻量化与动作分词优化入手，平衡效率与性能。核心思路是从“通用感知”向“具身空间智能”深化，通过专用模块与高效编码，实现更精准、实时的灵巧操作。This perspective focuses on architectural innovations of VLA large models in dexterous manipulation, aiming to solve bottlenecks in spatial understanding, action accuracy, and reasoning efficiency of traditional models. The evolution path shifts from simply stacking data scale (e.g., GR-2) towards architectural decoupling and spatial enhancement: CogACT improves control accuracy through cognitive-action separation; TraceVLA introduces visual trajectory prompts to compensate for spatiotemporal memory gaps; SpatialVLA strengthens 3D geometric perception to overcome planar vision limitations; TinyVLA and FAST start from lightweighting and action tokenization optimization, respectively, balancing efficiency and performance. The core idea is to deepen from 'general perception' to 'embodied spatial intelligence', achieving more precise and real-time dexterous manipulation through specialized modules and efficient encoding.

TinyVLA: Toward Fast, Data-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA模型参数量大、推理慢且部署成本高的问题，探索在保持性能的同时实现极致轻量化。Addresses the issues of large parameter count, slow inference, and high deployment costs in existing VLA models, exploring extreme lightweighting while maintaining performance.
方法: Method: 将视觉语言模型压缩至1B参数以内，并配合Diffusion Policy解码器替代自回归生成，以加速动作输出。Compresses the vision-language model to under 1B parameters and pairs it with a Diffusion Policy decoder to replace autoregressive generation, accelerating action output.
推进: Advance: 在推理速度上超越OpenVLA 20倍，证明了无需海量数据堆砌也能实现高效的灵巧操作，为边缘部署提供新范式。Exceeds OpenVLA by 20 times in inference speed, proving that efficient dexterous manipulation can be achieved without stacking massive data, providing a new paradigm for edge deployment.

方法: Method: 基于3800万视频与500亿token进行预训练，采用生成式视频语言动作架构，利用视频生成辅助动作预测。Pre-trained on 38 million videos and 50 billion tokens, adopting a generative video-language-action architecture and utilizing video generation to assist action prediction.
推进: Advance: 在已知任务上达到97.7%的高成功率，验证了数据规模对提升泛化性的关键作用，但揭示了视频生成在物理动态模拟上的局限性。Achieves a high success rate of 97.7% on known tasks, validating the critical role of data scale in improving generalization, but revealing the limitations of video generation in simulating physical dynamics.

动机: Motivation: 解决传统VLA模型将连续动作空间强行离散化导致的精度损失，以及认知与行动耦合带来的优化困难。To address the accuracy loss caused by forcibly discretizing the continuous action space in traditional VLA models, as well as the optimization difficulties arising from the coupling of cognition and action.
方法: Method: 提出认知与行动解耦架构，利用VLM处理语义认知，配合专用扩散动作模块（DiT）进行精细动作生成。Propose a decoupled architecture of cognition and action, utilizing VLMs for semantic cognition and a dedicated diffusion action module (DiT) for fine-grained action generation.
推进: Advance: 在真机实验中成功率较OpenVLA提升55%，证明了专用动作建模架构在灵巧操作任务中的显著优势与必要性。Achieve a 55% improvement in success rate over OpenVLA in real-robot experiments, demonstrating the significant advantages and necessity of dedicated action modeling architectures for dexterous manipulation tasks.

动机: Motivation: 克服VLA模型缺乏长程时空记忆、决策过度依赖当前帧的缺陷，增强模型对复杂操作轨迹的空间感知能力。Overcome the defects of VLA models lacking long-range spatiotemporal memory and decision-making overly dependent on the current frame, thereby enhancing the model's spatial perception capabilities for complex manipulation trajectories.

FAST: Efficient Action Tokenization for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对自回归VLA模型中动作分词粗糙、高频数据相关性高导致难以学习精细灵巧操作的问题进行优化。Optimize the issues of coarse action tokenization in autoregressive VLA models and the high correlation in high-frequency data, which make learning fine-grained dexterous operations difficult.
方法: Method: 提出FAST分词器，结合DCT变换与BPE算法，将连续动作空间高效离散化，并构建通用分词器FAST+。Propose the FAST tokenizer, combining DCT transformation with the BPE algorithm to efficiently discretize the continuous action space, and construct a universal tokenizer FAST+.
推进: Advance: 使自回归模型在灵巧操作精度上媲美扩散模型，且训练速度提升5倍，平衡了离散化带来的精度损失与计算效率。Enable autoregressive models to match the dexterous manipulation accuracy of diffusion models while increasing training speed by 5x, balancing the accuracy loss from discretization with computational efficiency.

EXIMO: VLM Guided Exploration of VLA Policies
动机: Motivation: 为了解决VLA策略在真实世界中通过纯强化学习探索效率低下、试错成本过高的问题，试图利用VLM的长程规划能力指导原子技能执行。To address the inefficiency of pure reinforcement learning exploration and the excessively high trial-and-error costs of VLA policies in the real world, attempting to utilize the long-horizon planning capabilities of VLMs to guide atomic skill execution.
方法: Method: 构建EXIMO框架，利用VLM作为“监工”生成任务规划与成功判定信号，将VLM的推理过程蒸馏回VLA策略，并辅以RL微调。Constructing the EXIMO framework, utilizing VLM as a 'supervisor' to generate task planning and success judgment signals, distilling the VLM reasoning process back into the VLA policy, and supplementing with RL fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 验证了“大模型规划+小模型执行”的蒸馏范式能大幅降低样本复杂度，但揭示了该方法极度依赖高精度成功检测器，在缺乏上帝视角的真实场景中泛化性面临严峻挑战。Validating that the 'large model planning + small model execution' distillation paradigm can significantly reduce sample complexity, but revealing that this method is extremely dependent on high-precision success detectors, facing severe generalization challenges in real-world scenarios lacking a god's-eye view.

OrthoSkillVLA: Continual Skill Learning via Gradient-Informed Skill Subspace Adaptation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA持续学习方法忽视语义模块与动作模块异质性，导致在技能更新时出现灾难性遗忘或精度下降的瓶颈。Addressing the bottleneck where existing VLA continual learning methods ignore the heterogeneity between semantic and action modules, leading to catastrophic forgetting or accuracy degradation during skill updates.
方法: Method: 提出OrthoSkillVLA，利用梯度信息构建正交子空间约束，并引入MoE解码器与模块感知预算，分别优化VLM的语义容量与ActionHead的响应速度。Proposing OrthoSkillVLA, utilizing gradient information to construct orthogonal subspace constraints, and introducing MoE decoders and module-aware budgets to optimize the semantic capacity of VLMs and the response speed of ActionHead, respectively.
推进: Advance: 通过正交子空间约束有效平衡了模型的可塑性与稳定性，证明了针对VLA不同模块特性进行差异化参数更新比统一微调更能维持灵巧操作的长期性能。Effectively balancing model plasticity and stability through orthogonal subspace constraints, demonstrating that differentiated parameter updates tailored to the characteristics of different VLA modules are more effective than unified fine-tuning for maintaining long-term dexterous operation performance.

生成式数据与数字孪生Generative Data and Digital Twins

该方向旨在利用生成式AI与数字孪生技术解决具身智能中真实交互数据稀缺与物理仿真保真度不足的痛点。核心思路从早期单纯生成几何场景（如PhyScene），演进为利用扩散模型与视频世界模型生成高保真、物理合理的合成数据（如DexMimicGen, DreamGen）。近期研究进一步提出“数字表亲”概念，通过功能相似性而非几何完美性实现Sim-to-Real迁移（如ACDC），并探索动作驱动的世界模型与反事实轨迹生成（如WorldSample, Ctrl-World），试图以低成本合成数据替代昂贵实机试错，推动灵巧操作从原子技能向长程复杂任务泛化。This direction aims to leverage generative AI and digital twin technologies to address the pain points of scarce real-world interaction data and insufficient physical simulation fidelity in embodied intelligence. The core approach has evolved from early generation of purely geometric scenes (e.g., PhyScene) to utilizing diffusion models and video world models to generate high-fidelity, physically plausible synthetic data (e.g., DexMimicGen, DreamGen). Recent research further proposes the concept of 'digital cousins', achieving Sim-to-Real transfer through functional similarity rather than geometric perfection (e.g., ACDC), and exploring action-driven world models and counterfactual trajectory generation (e.g., WorldSample, Ctrl-World), attempting to replace expensive real-robot trial-and-error with low-cost synthetic data to promote the generalization of dexterous manipulation from atomic skills to long-horizon complex tasks.

动机: Motivation: 解决世界动作模型（WAM）因视频生成导致的推理延迟高、难以部署到实时灵巧操作系统的工程瓶颈。Addresses the engineering bottleneck of high inference latency and difficulty in deployment to real-time dexterous manipulation systems caused by video generation in World Action Models (WAM).

推进: Advance: 实现了推理速度与性能的平衡，在保持VLA架构轻量化的同时，通过“训练时世界模型”显著提升了长程任务的泛化能力，解决了实时性痛点。Achieves a balance between inference speed and performance; while maintaining a lightweight VLA architecture, it significantly enhances generalization capabilities for long-horizon tasks via "World Model at Training Time," solving the real-time pain point.

论文
动机: Motivation: 针对当前动作条件世界模型过度关注视频外观保真度而忽视物理真实性的问题，旨在建立更严格的仿真器保真度诊断标准。Aiming to establish stricter simulator fidelity diagnostic standards to address the issue where current action-conditioned World Models overly focus on video appearance fidelity while neglecting physical realism.
方法: Method: 提出WorldSimProbe，构建包含“动作 - 运动 - 交互”的细粒度评估链条，定位模型是在模拟物理规律还是在单纯视觉幻觉。Proposes WorldSimProbe, constructing a fine-grained evaluation chain containing "action-motion-interaction" to locate whether the model is simulating physical laws or merely generating visual hallucinations.
推进: Advance: 提供了区分“物理正确”与“视觉相似”的诊断工具，揭示了现有ACWM在物理因果性上的不足，为提升数字孪生的物理保真度提供了量化依据。Provides diagnostic tools to distinguish between "physically correct" and "visually similar," revealing the insufficiency of existing ACWM in physical causality and offering a quantitative basis for improving the physical fidelity of digital twins.

论文
动机: Motivation: 针对手术机器人领域昂贵动作标注数据稀缺的痛点，试图利用海量无标签视频挖掘视觉动力学先验以替代昂贵的实机试错。Aiming to leverage massive unlabeled video data to mine visual dynamics priors as a substitute for expensive real-world trial-and-error, addressing the pain point of scarce expensive action annotation data in the surgical robotics field.
方法: Method: 提出Surgical WAM，将世界模型与动作预测模块深度融合，在仿真环境SurRoL中利用视频预训练学习物理规律。Proposes Surgical WAM, deeply integrating World Models with action prediction modules to learn physical laws via video pre-training in the simulation environment SurRoL.
推进: Advance: 验证了“视频预训练”在手术领域的可行性，证明了利用无标签数据学习物理先验比单纯模仿动作更具数据效率，但尚未在真机上完全闭环。Validates the feasibility of "video pre-training" in the surgical domain, proving that learning physical priors from unlabeled data is more data-efficient than purely imitating actions, though full closed-loop implementation on real hardware has not yet been achieved.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 解决现有世界动作模型（WAM）只顾微观物理预测而缺乏宏观任务阶段感知的缺陷，以提升长程复杂任务的规划能力。Addresses the defect of existing World Action Models (WAM) focusing solely on micro-physical prediction while lacking macro-task phase awareness, aiming to enhance planning capabilities for long-horizon complex tasks.
方法: Method: 构建JEPA-WAM，采用两阶段策略：利用冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取特征，分别预测短期物理未来和阶段语义未来，并将语义状态注入视频生成流。Constructs JEPA-WAM, adopting a two-stage strategy: utilizing a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract features, separately predicting short-term physical futures and phase semantic futures, and injecting semantic states into the video generation stream.
推进: Advance: 证明了“宏观语义指导”对“微观物理控制”的必要性，通过解耦物理与语义预测，有效减少了外观干扰，提升了任务逻辑的连贯性。Proves the necessity of "macro-semantic guidance" for "micro-physical control"; by decoupling physical and semantic predictions, it effectively reduces appearance interference and improves the coherence of task logic.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 针对世界模型倾向于在失败动作下“幻觉”出成功未来的问题，旨在利用被忽视的失败数据来纠正模型的物理认知偏差。Aiming to correct the model's physical cognitive bias by utilizing overlooked failure data to address the issue where World Models tend to "hallucinate" successful futures under failed actions.
方法: Method: 提出FACT框架，强制模型学习失败动作的后果，屏蔽动作模仿标签，迫使模型仅关注动作与物理后果的因果关联而非成功示范。Proposes the FACT framework, forcing the model to learn the consequences of failed actions, masking action imitation labels, and compelling the model to focus solely on the causal link between actions and physical consequences rather than successful demonstrations.
推进: Advance: 通过“以毒攻毒”的数据策略显著降低了模型在未见任务中的盲目自信幻觉，提升了Sim-to-Real迁移的鲁棒性，使模型不再仅依赖成功样本。Significantly reduces the model's blind confidence hallucinations on unseen tasks through a "poison cures poison" data strategy, enhancing Sim-to-Real transfer robustness and ensuring the model no longer relies solely on successful samples.
—
论文
动机: Motivation: 直面具身智能领域长期存在的“通用”口号与真实数据匮乏、Sim2Real鸿沟巨大的矛盾，试图验证合成数据作为主力训练源在真实灵巧操作中的可行性。Directly addressing the long-standing contradiction in the embodied intelligence field between the 'universal' slogan and the scarcity of real data and the huge Sim2Real gap, this work attempts to verify the feasibility of synthetic data as the main training source in real dexterous manipulation.
方法: Method: 通过RoboSynChallenge设立混合评估赛道，强制基线模型（Transformer、Diffusion）在大规模合成数据上训练，并在真实物理环境中进行双机械臂操作测试。Through RoboSynChallenge, a mixed evaluation track is established, forcing baseline models (Transformer, Diffusion) to train on large-scale synthetic data and conduct dual-robot arm operation tests in real physical environments.
推进: Advance: 验证了合成数据驱动策略在真实世界中的迁移潜力，但揭示了当前方法对真实环境噪声的鲁棒性仍不足，且过度依赖合成数据比例可能导致泛化瓶颈。The transfer potential of synthetic data-driven policies in the real world is verified, but it reveals that current methods still lack robustness to real-world environmental noise, and over-reliance on synthetic data proportions may lead to generalization bottlenecks.

论文
动机: Motivation: 针对人机递物任务中真实交互数据稀缺及Sim-to-Real鸿沟问题，旨在利用生成式模型构建包含真实传感器噪声的高保真RGB-D序列以增强策略泛化。Aiming to address the scarcity of real interaction data and the Sim-to-Real gap in human-to-robot object passing tasks, this work seeks to utilize generative models to construct high-fidelity RGB-D sequences containing real sensor noise to enhance policy generalization.
方法: Method: 构建了包含真实噪声的Hand2Bot数据集，并提出PassGen模型生成具备物理合理性的RGB-D视频序列，将人脸微表情作为触发条件引入生成过程。The Hand2Bot dataset containing real noise is constructed, and the PassGen model is proposed to generate physically plausible RGB-D video sequences, introducing human facial micro-expressions as trigger conditions into the generation process.
推进: Advance: 证明了在UR5e上通过生成数据实现零样本迁移的有效性，特别是在抑制误触发方面优于纯合成数据，但受限于静态背景导致泛化能力不足。The effectiveness of zero-shot transfer using generated data on UR5e is demonstrated, particularly in suppressing false triggers compared to purely synthetic data, but generalization capability is limited by static backgrounds.

论文
动机: Motivation: 旨在揭示当前动作条件世界模型缺乏真实物理动力学理解、仅靠 2D 视觉模式匹配的“虚假泛化”本质，特别是跨实体泛化的瓶颈。Aiming to reveal the essence of 'false generalization' in current action-conditioned world models, which lack true physical dynamic understanding and rely solely on 2D visual pattern matching, particularly regarding the bottleneck of cross-embodiment generalization.
方法: Method: 构建包含未见机器人形态的测试集，通过像素级动作表示和时空对齐方案，系统评估模型在更换机器人外壳后的视频生成与物理预测能力。Constructing a test set containing unseen robotic morphologies, systematically evaluating the model's video generation and physical prediction capabilities after changing the robot shell through pixel-level action representation and spatiotemporal alignment schemes.
推进: Advance: 实证发现单纯堆砌数据或扩大模型规模无法解决跨实体泛化问题，必须依赖架构创新（如显式物理建模）而非仅靠视觉对齐，为数字孪生生成数据的真实性提出了严峻挑战。Empirically finding that simply piling up data or expanding model scale cannot solve cross-embodiment generalization problems; architectural innovation (such as explicit physical modeling) is required rather than relying solely on visual alignment, posing a severe challenge to the authenticity of digital twin-generated data.

论文
动机: Motivation: 试图解决人类视频数据（非结构化、乱码）与机器人物理动作（结构化、物理语言）之间的巨大域差异，挖掘人类数据中的通用物理动态。Attempting to bridge the huge domain gap between human video data (unstructured, garbled) and robotic physical actions (structured, physical language), mining general physical dynamics from human data.
方法: Method: 提出双重动作对齐（Dual Action Alignment）机制，将人类手部与机器人机械臂映射到统一的隐式空间，利用多视角重建和预处理实现弱监督到强信号的转化。Proposing a Dual Action Alignment mechanism, mapping human hands and robotic manipulators to a unified implicit space, utilizing multi-view reconstruction and preprocessing to achieve transformation from weak supervision to strong signals.
推进: Advance: 验证了 Scaling Law 在人类视频数据上的有效性，证明了通过隐式对齐挖掘物理动态的可行性，为利用低成本人类数据生成高保真灵巧操作合成数据开辟了新路径。Validating the effectiveness of the Scaling Law on human video data, proving the feasibility of mining physical dynamics through implicit alignment, opening a new path for generating high-fidelity dexterous manipulation synthetic data using low-cost human data.

RoboCasa: Large-Scale Simulation of Everyday Tasks for Generalist Robots
动机: Motivation: 针对通用机器人训练数据稀缺，试图利用生成式AI大规模构建包含复合任务的仿真数据集。Address the scarcity of training data for general-purpose robots by attempting to use generative AI to construct large-scale simulation datasets containing composite tasks.
方法: Method: 基于RoboSuite引擎，利用LLM生成复合任务指令，结合AI生成的物体资产，构建了包含10万条轨迹的RoboCasa数据集。Based on the RoboSuite engine, utilize LLMs to generate composite task instructions and combine them with AI-generated object assets to construct the RoboCasa dataset containing 100,000 trajectories.
推进: Advance: 证明了合成数据在原子技能上的缩放有效性，虽复合任务迁移率有限，但为大规模预训练提供了低成本数据源。Demonstrate the scaling effectiveness of synthetic data on atomic skills; although transfer rates for composite tasks are limited, it provides a low-cost data source for large-scale pre-training.

DexMimicGen: Automated Data Generation for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation via Imitation Learning
动机: Motivation: 针对双灵巧手机器人数据饥渴，试图用少量真人演示在仿真中克隆出海量高质量数据。Address the data hunger of dual-dexterous-hand robots by attempting to clone massive amounts of high-quality data in simulation using a small number of human demonstrations.
方法: Method: 提出DexMimicGen框架，基于少量真机演示，利用SE(3)变换在仿真中生成大规模多样化操作数据。Propose the DexMimicGen framework, which utilizes SE(3) transformations in simulation to generate large-scale, diverse manipulation data based on a few real-robot demonstrations.
推进: Advance: 实现了60个演示生成21K数据，真机部署成功率达90%，证明了基于位姿变换的仿真数据增强策略的高效性。Achieve the generation of 21,000 data points from 60 demonstrations, with a real-robot deployment success rate of 90%, proving the efficiency of simulation data augmentation strategies based on pose transformations.

动机: Motivation: 利用视频世界模型生成合成数据，以解决机器人策略在长程任务和复杂环境下的泛化难题。Utilize video world models to generate synthetic data to address the generalization challenges of robot policies in long-horizon tasks and complex environments.
方法: Method: 通过微调视频扩散模型生成视频，利用逆动力学模型（IDM）反推动作，训练端到端策略。Generate videos by fine-tuning video diffusion models and reverse-engineer actions using an inverse dynamics model (IDM) to train end-to-end policies.
推进: Advance: 实现了仅用抓取放置数据训练出22种新任务的能力，证明了视频世界模型作为数据生成器的巨大潜力。Achieve the ability to train 22 new tasks using only pick-and-place data, demonstrating the immense potential of video world models as data generators.

动机: Motivation: 针对真实世界灵巧操作数据稀缺且物理仿真难以还原动态交互物体（如门）的痛点，旨在解决传统方法无法从单目视频高效构建具备物理属性的数字孪生体的问题。Aiming to address the pain point of scarce real-world dexterous operation data and the difficulty of physical simulation in restoring dynamic interactive objects (such as doors), and to solve the problem that traditional methods cannot efficiently build digital twins with physical attributes from monocular video.
方法: Method: 提出Video2DoorTraversal框架，通过ArticuACT策略结合Plücker射线与交互状态监督，从单视频重建带物理属性的门（DoorTwin），并在仿真中训练后迁移至真机。Proposing the Video2DoorTraversal framework, combining ArticuACT strategy with Plücker rays and interaction state supervision to reconstruct doors with physical attributes (DoorTwin) from a single video, and training in simulation before transferring to real machines.
推进: Advance: 突破了仅生成刚性物体的局限，实现了从单视频到动态物理孪生体的端到端构建，在推门任务中达到96%的成功率，验证了Real-to-Sim-to-Real流水线在复杂接触任务中的有效性。Breaking through the limitation of generating only rigid objects, achieving end-to-end construction from single video to dynamic physical twins, reaching a 96% success rate in door-pushing tasks, and validating the effectiveness of the Real-to-Sim-to-Real pipeline in complex contact tasks.

Autonomously Acquiring Robot Manipulation Skills with Language-Driven Quality-Diversity
动机: Motivation: 针对传统质量多样性（QD）算法依赖专家设计指标、难以自动化生成多样化操作原语库的痛点，利用大语言模型作为“裁判”来驱动技能探索。Addressing the pain points of traditional Quality Diversity (QD) algorithms that rely on expert-designed metrics and struggle to automatically generate diverse manipulation primitive libraries, this work leverages large language models as 'judges' to drive skill exploration.
方法: Method: 将LLM集成至QD框架中，使其能根据自然语言描述自动生成运动原语库和评估指标，并在Genesis仿真环境中进行自动化技能获取。Integrating LLMs into the QD framework enables the automatic generation of motion primitive libraries and evaluation metrics based on natural language descriptions, facilitating automated skill acquisition within the Genesis simulation environment.
推进: Advance: 实现了从自然语言到运动原语的端到端自动化，摆脱了对人工设计指标和规则库的依赖，为数字孪生环境下的低成本技能泛化提供了新范式。This approach achieves end-to-end automation from natural language to motion primitives, eliminating dependence on manually designed metrics and rule libraries, thereby providing a new paradigm for low-cost skill generalization in digital twin environments.

Behavior-Skill: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Policies in Long-Horizon Tasks
动机: Motivation: 指出当前长 horizon 任务评测过于关注任务级结果而忽视过程细节，导致难以精准定位具身智能在复杂灵巧操作中的具体失败原因。It points out that current long-horizon task evaluations overly focus on task-level outcomes while neglecting process details, making it difficult to precisely identify the specific causes of failure for embodied intelligence in complex dexterous manipulation.
方法: Method: 构建Behavior-Skill细粒度基准，将长序列任务拆解为原子技能单元，并基于大规模仿真数据（23万实例）对VLA策略进行技能级评估。A Behavior-Skill fine-grained benchmark is constructed by decomposing long-sequence tasks into atomic skill units, conducting skill-level evaluation of VLA policies based on large-scale simulation data (230,000 instances).
推进: Advance: 揭示了即便在数据丰富的仿真环境中，主流模型在原子技能层面的成功率仍不足50%，证明了仅靠增加数据量无法解决物理交互的深层鲁棒性问题。It reveals that even in data-rich simulation environments, the success rate of mainstream models at the atomic skill level remains below 50%, demonstrating that simply increasing data volume cannot resolve deep robustness issues in physical interaction.

扩散策略与3D表示融合Fusion of diffusion policies and 3D representations

本方向致力于解决具身智能中灵巧操作对高维3D空间理解与复杂动作分布建模的双重挑战。核心思路是将显式的3D几何表示（如点云、体素或Tokenized场景）直接嵌入扩散策略框架，利用其强大的多模态生成能力处理动作的不确定性。演进脉络从早期的简单特征融合（3D Diffuser Actor），发展到高效的向量压缩与推理加速（DP3, FlowPolicy），再到构建大规模双臂操作基础模型（RDT-1B）及优化微调策略（DPPO）。这一过程体现了从“验证3D表示有效性”向“追求实时性、泛化性与工程落地”的深刻转变，逐步克服扩散模型推理慢、样本效率低及长视界任务难的瓶颈。This direction aims to address the dual challenges of high-dimensional 3D spatial understanding and complex action distribution modeling in embodied intelligence for dexterous manipulation. The core approach involves embedding explicit 3D geometric representations (such as point clouds, voxels, or tokenized scenes) directly into the diffusion policy framework, leveraging their powerful multimodal generation capabilities to handle action uncertainty. The evolution has progressed from early simple feature fusion (3D Diffuser Actor), to efficient vector compression and inference acceleration (DP3, FlowPolicy), and further to constructing large-scale bimanual operation foundation models (RDT-1B) and optimizing fine-tuning strategies (DPPO). This process reflects a profound shift from "validating the effectiveness of 3D representations" to "pursuing real-time performance, generalization, and engineering deployment," gradually overcoming bottlenecks such as slow diffusion model inference, low sample efficiency, and difficulties in long-horizon tasks.

2024-02
3D Diffuser Actor: Policy Diffusion with 3D Scene Representations
动机: Motivation: 旨在验证将显式3D场景Token化表示直接融入扩散策略框架，能否显著提升机器人在复杂空间任务中的鲁棒性与性能。Aiming to verify whether directly incorporating explicit 3D scene tokenized representations into the diffusion policy framework can significantly enhance the robustness and performance of robots in complex spatial tasks.
方法: Method: 提出3D Diffuser Actor，利用Transformer将3D Token作为去噪过程的输入，替代传统的图像或本体感知嵌入。Proposing 3D Diffuser Actor, which utilizes Transformers to input 3D Tokens as the denoising process input, replacing traditional image or proprioceptive embeddings.
推进: Advance: 在RLBench和CALVIN基准上显著超越Act3D，证明了显式3D空间结构建模比隐式嵌入更具鲁棒性，尽管牺牲了部分推理速度。Significantly outperforming Act3D on RLBench and CALVIN benchmarks, proving that explicit 3D spatial structure modeling is more robust than implicit embeddings, despite sacrificing some inference speed.

3D Diffusion Policy
动机: Motivation: 针对现有3D扩散方法推理缓慢且难以在长视界任务中保持高效的问题，探索更紧凑的3D表示与扩散策略的融合。Addressing the issues of slow inference in existing 3D diffusion methods and the difficulty in maintaining efficiency in long-horizon tasks, exploring the fusion of more compact 3D representations with diffusion policies.
方法: Method: 提出3D Diffusion Policy，将点云压缩为64维向量直接输入扩散策略网络，并在72个仿真任务中进行大规模评估。Proposing 3D Diffusion Policy, which compresses point clouds into 64-dimensional vectors for direct input into the diffusion policy network, conducting large-scale evaluation across 72 simulation tasks.
推进: Advance: 实现了比原版Diffusion Policy快得多的推理速度且无性能损失，同时在灵巧抓取任务中展现出零违规的安全特性。Achieving significantly faster inference speed than the original Diffusion Policy without performance loss, while demonstrating zero-violation safety characteristics in dexterous grasping tasks.
2024-09
Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization
动机: Motivation: 解决扩散策略在微调阶段难以利用策略梯度进行高效优化的问题，特别是针对长视界任务的稳定性需求。Addressing the difficulty of diffusion policies in utilizing policy gradients for efficient optimization during the fine-tuning phase, particularly regarding stability requirements for long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 提出Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization (DPPO)，将策略梯度方法应用于扩散策略的微调过程，替代传统的Q-learning。Proposing Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization (DPPO), applying policy gradient methods to the fine-tuning process of diffusion policies, replacing traditional Q-learning.
推进: Advance: 证明了PG方法在扩散策略微调中同样有效且比Off-policy方法更稳定，为预训练模型的快速适应提供了实用框架。Demonstrating that PG methods are equally effective and more stable than off-policy methods in diffusion policy fine-tuning, providing a practical framework for rapid adaptation of pre-trained models.

RDT-1B: a Diffusion Foundation Model for Bimanual Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 试图构建一个能统一不同机器人硬件、支持双臂复杂操作的大规模扩散基础模型，解决多机器人预训练的对齐难题。Attempting to construct a large-scale diffusion foundation model capable of unifying different robot hardware and supporting complex bimanual operations, solving the alignment challenges in multi-robot pre-training.
方法: Method: 提出RDT-1B，利用1B参数扩散模型和物理可解释的统一动作空间，在1M数据上预训练并在特定硬件上微调。Proposing RDT-1B, utilizing a 1B parameter diffusion model and a physically interpretable unified action space, pre-trained on 1M data and fine-tuned on specific hardware.
推进: Advance: 实现了双臂操作任务的SOTA性能，展示了统一动作空间在跨机器人泛化中的关键作用，尽管训练成本极高。Achieving SOTA performance in bimanual manipulation tasks, demonstrating the critical role of unified action spaces in cross-robot generalization, despite extremely high training costs.
2024-12
FlowPolicy: Enabling Fast and Robust 3D Flow-based Policy via Consistency Flow Matching for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在3D灵巧操作中推理延迟高、难以满足实时控制需求的问题，旨在探索更高效的生成机制。Aiming to address the high inference latency of diffusion policies in 3D dexterous manipulation and their difficulty in meeting real-time control requirements, this work explores more efficient generation mechanisms.
方法: Method: 将3D点云条件直接嵌入一致性流匹配（Consistency Flow Matching）框架，替代传统扩散采样器，实现一步或极少步数生成动作。Directly embedding 3D point cloud conditions into the Consistency Flow Matching framework replaces traditional diffusion samplers, enabling action generation in one or very few steps.
推进: Advance: 成功将推理时间从145ms压缩至20ms，在保持与DP3相当成功率的同时，显著提升了3D扩散策略的实时性与工程落地潜力。Successfully compressing inference time from 145ms to 20ms, while maintaining success rates comparable to DP3, significantly enhances the real-time performance and engineering deployment potential of 3D diffusion policies.
2026-08
DreamHand: Repurposing Video Diffusion Models for Occlusion-Robust Egocentric 3D Hand Motion Recovery
动机: Motivation: 针对具身智能中灵巧操作面临的高遮挡与手出框等极端感知挑战，以及传统扩散模型多步采样导致推理延迟过高的问题，亟需一种能利用视频生成先验实现单步高效推理的3D手部姿态估计方案。Addressing the extreme perception challenges of high occlusion and hands out-of-frame in embodied dexterous operations, as well as the problem of excessively high inference latency caused by multi-step sampling in traditional diffusion models, urgently requiring a 3D hand pose estimation scheme that can leverage video generation priors for single-step efficient inference.
方法: Method: 提出将视频扩散模型重构为编码器架构，通过单前向传播直接预测3D手部轨迹，利用预训练视频生成模型的时序先验来隐式学习相机几何规律，从而在无需显式内参的情况下实现鲁棒感知。Proposing to reconstruct video diffusion models into an encoder architecture to directly predict 3D hand trajectories via a single forward pass, utilizing the temporal priors of pre-trained video generation models to implicitly learn camera geometric laws, thereby achieving robust perception without explicit intrinsic parameters.
推进: Advance: 成功将遮挡场景下的MPJPE误差降低30%-40%，并将推理速度提升33倍，证明了利用视频扩散先验替代传统多步扩散采样在离线3D感知任务中的巨大潜力。Successfully reducing MPJPE error in occluded scenarios by 30%-40% and increasing inference speed by 33 times, demonstrating the huge potential of leveraging video diffusion priors to replace traditional multi-step diffusion sampling in offline 3D perception tasks.
2026-08
RoMAN-Flow: Taming Autoregressive Normalizing Flows for Offline Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决扩散策略在离线强化学习中样本效率与推理延迟的矛盾，同时探索自回归归一化流（AR-NF）在具身控制中精确似然建模的可行性，试图突破传统扩散模型在实时闭环控制中的瓶颈。To address the contradiction between sample efficiency and inference latency in diffusion policies for offline reinforcement learning, while exploring the feasibility of autoregressive normalizing flows (AR-NF) for precise likelihood modeling in embodied control, attempting to break through the bottleneck of traditional diffusion models in real-time closed-loop control.
方法: Method: 设计NF-IQL算法避免训练时的自回归采样，并通过蒸馏技术将慢速的AR-NF压缩为单步生成器，结合向量压缩与推理加速技术，实现高效的策略执行。Designing the NF-IQL algorithm to avoid autoregressive sampling during training, and compressing the slow AR-NF into a single-step generator via distillation techniques, combined with vector compression and inference acceleration technologies to achieve efficient policy execution.
推进: Advance: 验证了AR-NF在离线RL中的有效性，在保持与π0相当性能的同时显著降低了推理延迟，为对实时性要求严苛的灵巧操作任务提供了新的策略建模范式。Validating the effectiveness of AR-NF in offline RL, significantly reducing inference latency while maintaining performance comparable to π0, providing a new policy modeling paradigm for dexterous operation tasks with stringent real-time requirements.

基准测试与评估体系Benchmarking and Evaluation Systems

Manipulation & Dexterous 领域的基准测试正从单一任务验证转向泛化性、鲁棒性与安全性的多维评估。早期工作如 FMB 确立了功能组装的标准化，随后 THE COLOSSEUM 和 LIBERO-Plus 揭示了模型在环境扰动下的脆弱性，推动评估向分布外（OOD）场景演进。近期研究如 RoboTwin 系列和 WatchAct 利用生成式数字孪生解决数据稀缺，并引入行为 grounding 测试长程推理。同时，HT-Bench 和 SurgVLA-Bench 分别填补了触觉表征与医疗手术场景的空白。整体演进逻辑是从“能否完成任务”转向“在何种复杂条件下、以何种安全方式、基于何种感知模态可靠地完成任务”，强调仿真与真机的相关性验证及长尾问题的暴露。Benchmarking in the Manipulation & Dexterous domain is shifting from single-task verification to multi-dimensional evaluation of generalization, robustness, and safety. Early work like FMB established standardization for functional assembly, followed by THE COLOSSEUM and LIBERO-Plus revealing model fragility under environmental perturbations, driving evaluation towards out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Recent studies such as the RoboTwin series and WatchAct utilize generative digital twins to address data scarcity and introduce behavior grounding tests for long-horizon reasoning. Meanwhile, HT-Bench and SurgVLA-Bench fill gaps in tactile representation and medical surgical scenarios, respectively. The overall evolutionary logic shifts from "whether tasks can be completed" to "under what complex conditions, in what safe manner, and based on what perceptual modalities tasks can be reliably completed," emphasizing the validation of simulation-to-reality correlation and the exposure of long-tail problems.

2024-02
THE COLOSSEUM: A Benchmark for Evaluating Generalization for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有策略在真实世界环境扰动下的脆弱性，亟需一个能模拟多重干扰的严苛评估场。Addressing the fragility of existing policies under real-world environmental perturbations, urgently requiring a rigorous evaluation field capable of simulating multiple interferences.
方法: Method: 提出 THE COLOSSEUM 基准，引入 14 种环境扰动（如遮挡、光照变化）并验证仿真与真机的相关性。Proposing the THE COLOSSEUM benchmark, introducing 14 types of environmental perturbations (such as occlusion and lighting changes) and validating the correlation between simulation and reality.
推进: Advance: 量化了 SOTA 模型在叠加扰动下的性能崩塌，证明了仿真评估对分布偏移的敏感性，但暴露了 RL 方法在该基准上的缺失。Quantifying the performance collapse of SOTA models under叠加 perturbations, proving the sensitivity of simulation evaluation to distribution shifts, but exposing the absence of RL methods on this benchmark.

推进: Advance: 证实了当前端到端 RL 在人形机器人上的局限性，表明分层强化学习（HRL）是解决长程规划的有效路径，但仿真与真机仍有差距。Confirming the limitations of current end-to-end RL on humanoid robots, indicating that hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) is an effective path for solving long-horizon planning, though a gap remains between simulation and reality.

Evaluating Real-World Robot Manipulation Policies in Simulation

方法: Method: 提出 SIMPLER 框架，通过调整纹理与物理参数追求仿真与真机表现的相关性而非完美数字孪生。Proposing the SIMPLER framework, pursuing correlation between simulation and reality performance by adjusting textures and physical parameters rather than perfect digital twins.

RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 路由系统中模型选择评估过于复杂且缺乏有效监督信号的问题。Address the issue of overly complex model selection evaluation in VLA routing systems and the lack of effective supervision signals.

ForesightSafety-VLA: A Unified Diagnostic Safety Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 现有评估仅关注任务完成度，缺乏对 VLA 模型在物理交互中安全性（如碰撞、违规）的系统性度量。Existing evaluations focus solely on task completion, lacking a systematic measure of VLA model safety (e.g., collisions, violations) during physical interaction.
方法: Method: 构建 ForesightSafety-VLA，将安全拆解为物理、指令、感知维度，引入累积安全成本与风险暴露时间指标。Construct ForesightSafety-VLA, decomposing safety into physical, instruction, and perception dimensions, and introducing metrics for cumulative safety cost and risk exposure time.
推进: Advance: 提供了首个统一的安全诊断基准，揭示了最强模型仍存在“侥幸违规”现象，但仿真环境对真实物理风险的还原度仍存疑。Provided the first unified safety diagnosis benchmark, revealing that even the strongest models still exhibit 'lucky violations', though the fidelity of simulation environments to real physical risks remains questionable.
2026-06
WatchAct: A Benchmark for Behavior-Grounded Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对 VLA 模型缺乏对“行为上下文”理解能力，导致无法处理长程推理与动态环境变化的问题。Address the lack of VLA model understanding of 'behavioral context', leading to an inability to handle long-horizon reasoning and dynamic environmental changes.
方法: Method: 提出 WatchAct 基准，要求机器人基于人类行为视频进行规划，测试其从“看图说话”到“看人干活”的跨越。Propose the WatchAct benchmark, requiring robots to plan based on human behavior videos, testing the leap from 'describing images' to 'watching people work'.
推进: Advance: 照出了当前 VLA 在长程推理与物理执行上的双重短板，证明了仅靠指令不足以支撑复杂操作，需引入行为 grounding。Exposed the dual shortcomings of current VLAs in long-horizon reasoning and physical execution, proving that instructions alone are insufficient to support complex operations, necessitating the introduction of behavioral grounding.
2026-06
HT-Bench: Benchmarking and Learning Dexterous Full-Hand Tactile Representations with Egocentric Vision
动机: Motivation: 解决灵巧手触觉表征学习缺乏统一基准与大规模数据，导致触觉模态在操作任务中难以发挥作用的痛点。Address the pain point that the lack of a unified benchmark and large-scale data for dexterous hand tactile representation learning makes it difficult for the tactile modality to play a role in manipulation tasks.
方法: Method: 构建 HT-Bench，提供大规模 RGB-触觉数据，设计 HandTouch 模型进行多任务表征学习评测。Construct HT-Bench, providing large-scale RGB-tactile data and designing the HandTouch model for multi-task representation learning evaluation.
推进: Advance: 确立了触觉表征学习的多维度评测标准，验证了跨模态掩码机制的有效性，但受限于特定传感器配置且未上真机验证。Established a multi-dimensional evaluation standard for tactile representation learning, validated the effectiveness of the cross-modal masking mechanism, but remains limited by specific sensor configurations and lacks real-robot validation.

DexVerse: A Modular Benchmark for Multi-Task, Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对灵巧操作基准测试长期存在的任务单一、机器人形态固定及评估维度不足问题，旨在终结内卷并建立涵盖多任务、多具身形态的标准化评估体系。Aiming to address the long-standing issues of single tasks, fixed robot morphologies, and insufficient evaluation dimensions in dexterous manipulation benchmark tests, this work seeks to end the internal involution and establish a standardized evaluation system covering multiple tasks and embodied morphologies.
方法: Method: 构建了包含100个多样化任务的模块化基准，整合多种机械臂形态与视觉变体，并利用VR遥操作采集高质量接触力数据。We construct a modular benchmark containing 100 diverse tasks, integrating various robotic arm morphologies and visual variants, and utilizing VR teleoperation to collect high-quality contact force data.
推进: Advance: 实证揭示了现有SOTA模型在灵巧操作上的巨大性能鸿沟（平均成功率仅34%），并发现扩散策略在亚毫米级对齐与力控鲁棒性上优于VLA预训练模型。Empirical results reveal a huge performance gap in existing SOTA models for dexterous manipulation (average success rate only 34%), and we find that diffusion policies outperform VLA pre-trained models in sub-millimeter alignment and force control robustness.

Artificial Foveated Perception for Mitigating Shortcut Learning in Robotic Foundation Models
动机: Motivation: 针对现有机器人基础模型在微调阶段依赖背景捷径导致泛化性差、分布外（OOD）场景下表现脆弱的问题，亟需一种能强制模型聚焦关键物体特征的评估与优化机制。Addressing the issue that existing robot foundation models rely on background shortcuts during fine-tuning, leading to poor generalization and fragility in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, there is an urgent need for an evaluation and optimization mechanism that forces the model to focus on key object features.
方法: Method: 提出人工黄斑感知（AFP）框架，在微调阶段引入辅助损失函数引导注意力机制聚焦物体，并在推理阶段移除该模块以零成本部署。Proposes the Artificial Foveal Perception (AFP) framework, which introduces an auxiliary loss function during the fine-tuning phase to guide the attention mechanism towards objects, and removes this module during the inference phase for zero-cost deployment.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了模型在长程任务中的OOD成功率，揭示了注意力引导是解决基准测试中‘走捷径’现象的关键，为评估模型真实推理能力提供了新的验证维度。Significantly improved the OOD success rate of models in long-horizon tasks, revealing that attention guidance is key to solving the 'shortcut' phenomenon in benchmark tests and providing a new validation dimension for assessing the model's true reasoning capabilities.

DeepInsight II: One Trace from Benchmark to Robot
动机: Motivation: 针对现有基准测试过度依赖仿真环境且缺乏真机一致性验证的问题，旨在解决评估体系与真实部署风险脱节的痛点。Aims to address the pain point of the disconnect between evaluation systems and real deployment risks, targeting the issue that existing benchmark tests overly rely on simulation environments and lack real-machine consistency validation.
方法: Method: 提出在仿真与真机中执行完全相同的操作轨迹（trace），并引入“可修复性”标准来量化硬件误差归因与系统鲁棒性。Proposes executing identical operation traces in both simulation and real machines, and introduces a 'repairability' standard to quantify hardware error attribution and system robustness.
推进: Advance: 打破了以往仅在仿真中刷分的惯例，通过基础设施的连续性验证揭示了模型在真实物理环境中的落地风险，证明了评估一致性比单一性能指标更能反映真实部署能力。Breaks the convention of merely scoring in simulation; through continuity verification of infrastructure, it reveals deployment risks of models in real physical environments, proving that evaluation consistency reflects real deployment capabilities better than single performance metrics.
