Robo-Dopamine 2.0: History-Conditioned and OOD-Aware Process Reward Modeling for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决长程操作任务中因误差累积导致的失败，以及传统评估难以区分静态前后状态与时序动态变化的问题。Aims to solve the problem of failure due to error accumulation in long-horizon operation tasks, as well as the difficulty for traditional evaluation to distinguish between static pre- and post-states and temporal dynamic changes.
方法: Method: 构建历史条件化与分布外（OOD）感知的过程奖励模型，利用符号化进度空间统一建模成功、鲁棒与失败状态，并采用成对（pairwise）预测接口优化策略。Constructs a process reward model with historical conditioning and out-of-distribution (OOD) perception, using a symbolic progress space to uniformly model success, robustness, and failure states, and adopting a pairwise prediction interface for strategy optimization.
推进: Advance: 将评估维度从单一的任务结果扩展至包含历史上下文的过程鲁棒性，有效缓解了长程操作中的时序歧义，并在真实世界实验中验证了其在复杂扰动下的泛化潜力。Expands evaluation dimensions from single task outcomes to include process robustness with historical context, effectively alleviating temporal ambiguity in long-horizon operations and validating its generalization potential under complex disturbances in real-world experiments.
2026-08
SoftVTBench: A Deformation-Aware Visuo-Tactile Dataset and Benchmark for Deformable-Object Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有基准仅关注任务完成度，忽视了物理交互中物体形变与安全性评估的缺失，导致“成功”定义片面。Existing benchmarks focus solely on task completion, neglecting the lack of object deformation and safety assessment in physical interactions, leading to a one-sided definition of 'success'.
方法: Method: 构建包含4000次演示和50+资产的软体物体数据集，引入基于FEM真值的DSR指标，强制评估策略在完成任务同时控制形变。Constructing a soft object dataset comprising 4000 demonstrations and 50+ assets, introducing the DSR metric based on FEM ground truth to enforce strategy evaluation on both task completion and deformation control.
推进: Advance: 揭示了任务完成度与物理安全性之间的巨大鸿沟，证明触觉融合虽能提升分布外鲁棒性但非万能，填补了形变感知评估空白。Revealing a significant gap between task completion and physical safety, proving that while tactile fusion improves out-of-distribution robustness, it is not a panacea, thereby filling the gap in deformation perception assessment.

Revisiting the "Push-T" Robot Manipulation Task with Agentic Robotics
动机: Motivation: 传统基准过度依赖人类演示数据，且难以验证大模型在纯几何推理与自进化下的泛化能力，缺乏对仿真与真机差异的审视。Traditional benchmarks overly rely on human demonstration data and struggle to verify the generalization capabilities of large models under pure geometric reasoning and self-evolution, lacking scrutiny of the sim-to-real gap.
方法: Method: 设计无需人类演示的Agentic框架，让LLM在仿真环境中通过自我试错将经典Push-T任务成功率从62.5%提升至100%。Designing an Agentic framework requiring no human demonstrations, enabling LLMs to improve the success rate of the classic Push-T task from 62.5% to 100% through self-exploration in simulation environments.
推进: Advance: 证明了大模型在理想物理引擎下的几何推理潜力，同时暴露了仿真器API漏洞可能被利用的风险，警示了从仿真到真机迁移的严峻挑战。Demonstrating the potential of large models for geometric reasoning under ideal physics engines, while exposing the risk that simulator API vulnerabilities could be exploited, highlighting the severe challenges of sim-to-real transfer.
2026-08
MANIGUARD: A Benchmark and Data Suite for Specification-Grounded Safety Evaluation and Improvement of Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有评估将“任务完成”等同于“安全”，掩盖了零样本策略在动态执行中可能存在的隐性违规与物理风险。Current evaluations equate 'task completion' with 'safety', masking potential implicit violations and physical risks in zero-shot strategies during dynamic execution.
方法: Method: 利用形式化逻辑（LTLf）构建ManiGuard基准，将安全评估转化为可自动执行的数学验证，并引入真机测试环节。Utilizing formal logic (LTLf) to construct the ManiGuard benchmark, transforming safety assessment into executable mathematical verification and introducing a real-robot testing phase.
推进: Advance: 成功解耦“成功”与“安全”指标，发现即便经过微调仍有大量活跃轨迹违规，有力反驳了单纯依靠数据Scaling解决安全问题的观点。Successfully decoupling 'success' and 'safety' metrics, discovering that even after fine-tuning, a significant number of active trajectories violate safety constraints, strongly refuting the view that data scaling alone solves safety issues.

多模态数据利用与视频学习Multimodal data utilization and video learning

该方向致力于解决机器人灵巧操作中的数据稀缺与泛化难题，核心思路是从互联网多模态视频（尤其是人类视频）中挖掘通用操作先验，跨越“人类手”到“机器人手”的形态鸿沟。演进路径从早期的遥操作数据积累（DROID），转向利用视频生成与光流作为跨域接口（Flow, Phantom），进而发展至通过关键点解耦（Point Policy）和触觉世界模型（Tactile-WAM）实现细粒度控制。最新趋势强调利用世界模型进行长程推理与评估（DreamVLA, RoboWorld），并探索事件相机与触觉融合以增强鲁棒性，最终目标是实现无需大量真机数据即可在复杂物理环境中泛化的具身智能系统。This direction aims to solve the problems of data scarcity and generalization in dexterous manipulation, with the core idea of mining general manipulation priors from internet multimodal videos (especially human videos) to bridge the morphological gap between 'human hands' and 'robot hands'. The evolutionary path has shifted from early teleoperation data accumulation (DROID) to utilizing video generation and optical flow as cross-domain interfaces (Flow, Phantom), and further developed to achieve fine-grained control through keypoint disentanglement (Point Policy) and tactile world models (Tactile-WAM). The latest trend emphasizes using world models for long-horizon reasoning and evaluation (DreamVLA, RoboWorld), and exploring the fusion of event cameras and tactile sensing to enhance robustness, with the ultimate goal of achieving embodied intelligence systems that can generalize in complex physical environments without requiring large amounts of real-robot data.

论文
动机: Motivation: 旨在纠正物体中心世界模型（OCWM）领域对“槽位机制”的过度崇拜，探究在灵巧操作任务中真正决定表征质量与泛化鲁棒性的核心因素。Aiming to correct the excessive worship of 'slot mechanisms' in the Object-Centric World Model (OCWM) field, this work explores the core factors that truly determine representation quality and generalization robustness in dexterous manipulation tasks.
方法: Method: 通过消融实验对比不同槽位绑定策略、辅助掩码目标及本体感知输入，并验证冻结预训练特征（DINO）与端到端全局 Token 在分布外场景下的表现差异。Ablation experiments are conducted to compare different slot binding strategies, auxiliary mask objectives, and proprioceptive inputs, verifying the performance differences between frozen pre-trained features (DINO) and end-to-end global tokens in out-of-distribution scenarios.
推进: Advance: 实证表明预训练特征（DINO）才是提升世界模型鲁棒性的关键护城河，而非物体中心架构本身，揭穿了 OCWM 在复杂场景下“虚胖”且小物体失效的真相。Empirical evidence shows that pre-trained features (DINO) are the key moat for improving world model robustness, rather than the object-centric architecture itself, exposing the truth that OCWM is 'puffed up' in complex scenarios and fails with small objects.

论文
动机: Motivation: 解决行为克隆（BC）在应对阴影、干扰物等域偏移时，传统随机增强破坏任务关键语义或现有叠加增强方法效果不佳的问题。Addressing the problem that traditional random augmentation destroys task-critical semantics or existing stacked augmentation methods perform poorly when Behavior Cloning (BC) faces domain shifts such as shadows and occlusions.
方法: Method: 提出 RoboSaGA 方法，利用显著性（Saliency）引导的增强策略，智能区分并保护任务关键区域，同时对非关键区域进行针对性扰动。The RoboSaGA method is proposed, utilizing saliency-guided augmentation strategies to intelligently distinguish and protect task-critical regions while applying targeted perturbations to non-critical regions.
推进: Advance: 成功将行为克隆的视觉域偏移性能差距压缩至极低水平，证明了在视频学习中实施“按需定制”的语义感知增强比通用随机增强更具泛化潜力。The visual domain shift performance gap of Behavior Cloning is successfully compressed to a very low level, demonstrating that 'on-demand' semantic-aware augmentation implemented in video learning has greater generalization potential than generic random augmentation.

论文
动机: Motivation: 针对现有视频生成模型在跨具身（Human-to-Robot）任务中过度追求视觉保真度而忽视物理交互逻辑与任务意图的痛点，揭示视觉质量与功能有效性之间的巨大鸿沟。Addressing the pain point that existing video generation models excessively pursue visual fidelity while neglecting physical interaction logic and task intent in cross-embodied (Human-to-Robot) tasks, revealing the huge gap between visual quality and functional effectiveness.
方法: Method: 提出 H2R-Bench 基准，强制视频生成模型在生成过程中保留人类示范中的任务意图与交互证据，而非仅进行风格迁移，并引入 MLLM 进行功能导向的评估。The H2R-Bench benchmark is proposed, forcing video generation models to retain task intent and interaction evidence from human demonstrations during generation, rather than merely performing style transfer, and introducing MLLMs for function-oriented evaluation.
推进: Advance: 颠覆了单纯以视频画质（VBench）为标准的评测范式，确立了“功能保真”优于“视觉保真”的具身视频生成新准则，指出当前模型在接触逻辑与手型适配上的根本缺陷。This subverts the evaluation paradigm based solely on video quality (VBench), establishing a new criterion for embodied video generation where 'functional fidelity' supersedes 'visual fidelity', pointing out fundamental defects in current models regarding contact logic and hand shape adaptation.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决人类视频数据与机器人操作动作之间存在的巨大形态鸿沟，试图通过隐式对齐将非结构化的多模态人类视频转化为机器人可理解的物理先验。Aiming to solve the huge morphological gap between human video data and robotic manipulation actions, attempting to transform unstructured multimodal human videos into physical priors understandable by robots through implicit alignment.
方法: Method: 提出双重动作对齐机制，将人类手部操作与机器人机械臂动作映射至统一的130维潜在空间，利用大规模人类视频进行弱监督预训练。Proposing a Dual Action Alignment mechanism, mapping human hand operations and robotic manipulator actions to a unified 130-dimensional latent space, utilizing large-scale human videos for weakly supervised pre-training.
推进: Advance: 验证了Scaling Law在人类视频数据上的有效性，证明了无需大量真机数据即可通过挖掘通用物理动态实现操作策略的泛化。Validating the effectiveness of the Scaling Law on human video data, proving that operation policy generalization can be achieved by mining general physical dynamics without requiring large amounts of real-robot data.

论文
动机: Motivation: 针对多视角视频数据中相机位姿变化导致策略学习困难的问题，试图消除视角差异对机器人空间几何感知的干扰。Addressing the difficulty in policy learning caused by camera pose variations in multi-view video data, attempting to eliminate the interference of viewpoint differences on the robot's spatial geometric perception.
方法: Method: 利用大型3D视觉模型（如VGGT）将RGB图像重建为点云并对齐至世界坐标系，渲染为固定视角的标准视图作为策略输入。Utilizing large-scale 3D vision models (such as VGGT) to reconstruct RGB images into point clouds and align them to the world coordinate system, rendering them as standard views at a fixed viewpoint as policy inputs.
推进: Advance: 实现了从“学习相机位置”到“学习物体位置”的范式转变，显著降低了策略对多视角数据的依赖，提升了在几何变换下的鲁棒性。Achieving a paradigm shift from 'learning camera positions' to 'learning object positions', significantly reducing the policy's dependence on multi-view data and enhancing robustness under geometric transformations.

动机: Motivation: 为了解决灵巧操作领域缺乏大规模、多样化真实世界数据的问题，以支持模型泛化。To address the lack of large-scale, diverse real-world data in the field of dexterous manipulation to support model generalization.
方法: Method: 构建了包含76k轨迹和564个场景的DROID数据集，涵盖Franka机械臂与Oculus遥操作采集的广泛场景。Constructed the DROID dataset containing 76k trajectories and 564 scenes, covering a wide range of scenarios collected by Franka manipulators and Oculus teleoperation.

Track2Act: Predicting Point Tracks from Internet Videos Enables Generalizable Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 旨在利用互联网视频中的丰富视觉信息，解决机器人操作数据稀缺且难以泛化的问题。Aims to utilize the rich visual information in internet videos to solve the problems of scarce robot manipulation data and difficulty in generalization.
方法: Method: 提出Track2Act，从视频中预测点轨迹并转换为3D刚体变换，仅需少量机器人数据微调。Propose Track2Act, predicting point trajectories from videos and converting them into 3D rigid body transformations, requiring only a small amount of robot data for fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 证明了点轨迹作为中间表示比直接预测动作或视频掩码更精准且计算高效，显著提升了未见物体和场景的泛化能力。Proved that point trajectories as an intermediate representation are more accurate and computationally efficient than directly predicting actions or video masks, significantly improving generalization capabilities for unseen objects and scenes.

动机: Motivation: 为了在稀疏标注数据下学习通用的机器人行为，降低对全标注数据的依赖。To learn general robot behaviors under sparsely labeled data, reducing dependence on fully labeled data.
方法: Method: 提出Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MDT)，利用多模态目标引导扩散策略，结合预训练CLIP和Voltron。Propose Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MDT), utilizing multi-modal object-guided diffusion policies, combining pre-trained CLIP and Voltron.
推进: Advance: 在CALVIN基准上实现了更长的rollout长度，证明了扩散策略在少样本场景下的有效性，尽管真机鲁棒性仍需提升。Achieved longer rollout lengths on the CALVIN benchmark, proving the effectiveness of diffusion policies in few-shot scenarios, although real-robot robustness still needs improvement.

Flow as the Cross-Domain Manipulation Interface
动机: Motivation: 试图消除仿真与真实世界之间的域差异，利用人类视频直接指导机器人操作。Attempts to eliminate the domain gap between simulation and the real world, utilizing human videos to directly guide robot manipulation.
方法: Method: 提出Flow作为跨域接口，利用物体光流连接人类视频与机器人仿真数据，无需真机训练。Propose Flow as a cross-domain interface, utilizing object optical flow to connect human videos with robot simulation data, requiring no real-robot training.

Phantom: Training Robots Without Robots Using Only Human Videos
动机: Motivation: 旨在完全摆脱对机器人演示数据的依赖，仅利用人类视频训练机器人策略。aims to completely eliminate dependence on robot demonstration data, training robot policies solely using human videos.
方法: Method: 提出Phantom，通过数据编辑技术将人类视频“重绘”为机器人视频，实现零机器人数据训练。proposes Phantom, employing data editing techniques to "redraw" human videos into robot videos, achieving zero-robot-data training.
推进: Advance: 在处理可变形物体和多物体操作上取得了高成功率，但受限于手姿估计精度和任务类型（如仅支持捏取）。achieves high success rates in handling deformable objects and multi-object manipulation, but is limited by hand pose estimation accuracy and task types (e.g., only supporting pinching).

DreamVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model Dreamed with Comprehensive World Knowledge
动机: Motivation: 为了降低世界模型在机器人操作中的计算成本，同时保持对物理动态的有效预测。to reduce the computational cost of world models in robot manipulation while maintaining effective prediction of physical dynamics.

FlowWAM: Optical Flow as a Unified Action Representation for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决世界动作模型中视觉生成与动作控制之间的模态鸿沟，试图寻找一种能统一视频生成与动作表示的中间形式。Aiming to bridge the modality gap between visual generation and action control in world action models, this work seeks an intermediate form that unifies video generation and action representation.
方法: Method: 提出 FlowWAM，利用光流（Optical Flow）作为统一的动作表示输入，直接驱动视频生成模型，省去复杂的动作 Tokenizer。We propose FlowWAM, utilizing optical flow as a unified action representation input to directly drive video generation models, eliminating the need for complex action Tokenizers.
推进: Advance: 验证了光流作为稠密动作表征的有效性，成功 bridging 了视频生成与机器人控制，在 RoboTwin 等基准上实现了 SOTA 性能。We validate the effectiveness of optical flow as a dense action representation, successfully bridging video generation and robot control, achieving SOTA performance on benchmarks such as RoboTwin.

ODEWorld: A Continuous Predictive Architecture via Physical-Time Flow
动机: Motivation: 为突破传统离散帧预测模型在时间连续性和物理平滑性上的局限，试图建立更符合物理时间连续演化的世界模型。To break through the limitations of traditional discrete frame prediction models in temporal continuity and physical smoothness, this work attempts to establish world models more aligned with the continuous evolution of physical time.
方法: Method: 提出 ODEWorld，利用常微分方程（ODE）求解器处理连续时间流，结合 JVP 监督机制防止表示坍塌。We propose ODEWorld, utilizing Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) solvers to handle continuous time flows and combining JVP supervision mechanisms to prevent representation collapse.
推进: Advance: 实现了任意分辨率的逆向生成与平滑预测，为具身智能提供了更连续、更符合物理规律的时间建模范式。We achieve inverse generation and smooth prediction at arbitrary resolutions, providing a more continuous and physically consistent temporal modeling paradigm for embodied intelligence.

SiMDex: Mining Similar Egocentric Videos for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对互联网多模态视频数据中海量噪声导致灵巧操作训练效率低下的问题，旨在解决如何从海量人类视频中精准筛选出对机器人跨形态迁移真正有效的操作样本。Addressing the problem of low training efficiency for dexterous manipulation caused by massive noise in internet multi-modal video data, we aim to solve how to precisely filter operation samples from massive human videos that are truly effective for robot cross-embodiment transfer.
方法: Method: 构建基于运动学相似性的工业级推荐系统，利用光流重排序技术从 3200 万视频库中挖掘 150 万高价值自第一人称视角视频，并直接用于 VLA 模型的后训练。We construct an industrial-grade recommendation system based on kinematic similarity, utilizing optical flow reordering to mine 1.5 million high-value first-person-view videos from a 32 million video library for direct post-training of VLA models.
推进: Advance: 证明了数据筛选策略比单纯堆砌数据量更能显著提升跨形态灵巧操作成功率，将成功率从 47.7% 提升至 61.1%，确立了以数据质量为核心的训练范式。We prove that data filtering strategies significantly improve cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation success rates more effectively than simply stacking data volume, increasing success rates from 47.7% to 61.1%, thereby establishing a training paradigm centered on data quality.

方法: Method: 采用模块化架构，将 LLMDet、SAMv2、DINOv2 与 GeoTransformer 等预训练大模型进行“即插即用”式组合，构建无需特定环境微调的端到端感知与规划系统。We adopt a modular architecture to combine pre-trained large models such as LLMDet, SAMv2, DINOv2, and GeoTransformer in a "plug-and-play" manner, constructing an end-to-end perception and planning system that requires no specific environment fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 验证了多模态基础模型组合在复杂非结构化环境中的泛化潜力，在无需 CAD 先验知识的情况下实现了 89.12% 的物体检测精度，为数据稀缺场景提供了工程化解决方案。We validate the generalization potential of multi-modal foundation model combinations in complex unstructured environments, achieving 89.12% object detection accuracy without CAD prior knowledge, providing an engineering solution for data-scarce scenarios.

推进: Advance: 开创了利用无标签视频数据“喂饱”数据饥渴的模仿学习新路径，有效缓解了真机数据获取难题，验证了弱监督策略在提升模型OOD泛化性上的可行性。Pioneers a new path of 'feeding' data-hungry imitation learning with unlabeled video data, effectively alleviating the difficulty of obtaining real-machine data and validating the feasibility of weakly supervised strategies in improving model OOD generalization.

ZimaBlue: Evolving Generalizable World Action Models through Scalable Video Pre-training
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人灵巧操作数据稀缺及真机训练成本高昂的痛点，旨在利用海量互联网视频数据挖掘通用操作先验，以跨越人类与机器人形态差异。Aiming to address the scarcity of robot dexterous manipulation data and the high cost of real-robot training, this work seeks to mine general manipulation priors from massive internet videos to bridge the morphological gap between humans and robots.
方法: Method: 提出三阶段训练框架，通过大规模视频预训练构建世界动作模型，结合异步推理架构（Slow-Fast模型）实现从纯视频观察到具身控制的迁移。A three-stage training framework is proposed, constructing a world action model through large-scale video pre-training and combining an asynchronous inference architecture (Slow-Fast model) to achieve transfer from pure video observation to embodied control.
推进: Advance: 成功将操作成功率从36%提升至77%，验证了Scaling Law在视频驱动机器人控制中的有效性，证明了利用生成式视频模型学习物理交互先验的可行性。Manipulation success rates were successfully improved from 36% to 77%, validating the effectiveness of Scaling Laws in video-driven robot control and demonstrating the feasibility of learning physical interaction priors using generative video models.

Learning to infer and manipulate through distributed whole-arm interaction in a soft robot
动机: Motivation: 为解决视觉依赖导致的感知局限及复杂物理接触下的数据标注难题，探索利用软体机器人本体形变作为环境信息的直接编码源。To address the perceptual limitations caused by visual dependence and the challenges of data annotation under complex physical contact, this work explores utilizing soft robot body deformation as a direct encoding source for environmental information.
方法: Method: 构建分布式全臂交互学习框架，将触觉与本体感觉融合，使机器人无需视觉输入即可通过身体形变推断环境状态并执行操作。A distributed full-arm interaction learning framework is constructed, fusing tactile and proprioceptive feedback, enabling robots to infer environmental states and execute operations through body deformation without visual input.
推进: Advance: 揭示了软体结构形变本身即可作为有效的环境感知模态，在特定任务中实现了无需视觉的高鲁棒性操作，为多模态数据利用提供了非视觉维度的新范式。It reveals that soft structure deformation itself can serve as an effective environmental perception modality, achieving high-robustness operation without vision in specific tasks, providing a new non-visual dimension paradigm for multi-modal data utilization.

混合学习与人机协作Hybrid learning and human-robot collaboration

在混合学习与人机协作的视角下，灵巧操作领域正从单纯依赖人类演示或纯仿真训练，转向“人机共驾”与“数据蒸馏”的深度融合。核心思路是利用人类的高层意图与低层鲁棒性，结合强化学习在仿真中的试错能力，解决真实世界数据稀缺、物理交互复杂及长程任务规划难的问题。演进路径表现为：从早期的静态交接和遥操作，发展到利用 sEMG 等低成本传感器增强力觉反馈，再到利用 VLA 模型与语义奖励进行策略蒸馏与对齐。最新趋势强调在开放世界中，通过人机实时交互（HIL）修正策略，利用异步架构解决长程任务，并关注从技术指标向人类感知体验的转化，旨在构建既具备高泛化能力又符合人类直觉的具身智能系统。From the perspective of hybrid learning and human-robot collaboration, the field of dexterous manipulation is shifting from relying solely on human demonstrations or pure simulation training towards a deep integration of 'human-robot co-driving' and 'data distillation'. The core idea leverages human high-level intent and low-level robustness, combined with the trial-and-error capabilities of reinforcement learning in simulation, to address challenges such as data scarcity in the real world, complex physical interactions, and difficulties in long-horizon task planning. The evolutionary path manifests as: from early static handovers and teleoperation, to enhancing force feedback using low-cost sensors like sEMG, and further to strategy distillation and alignment using VLA models and semantic rewards. The latest trends emphasize correcting policies through real-time human-in-the-loop (HIL) interaction in open worlds, utilizing asynchronous architectures to handle long-horizon tasks, and focusing on the transition from technical metrics to human perceptual experience, aiming to build embodied intelligent systems that possess high generalization capabilities while aligning with human intuition.

方法: Method: 提出集中训练分散执行（CTDE）架构结合混合奖励架构（HRA），利用人类专家干预作为稀疏奖励的补充信号，实现策略在真实物理环境中的在线优化。Proposes a Centralized Training Decentralized Execution (CTDE) architecture combined with a Hybrid Reward Architecture (HRA), utilizing human expert intervention as a supplementary signal for sparse rewards to achieve online optimization of policies in real physical environments.
推进: Advance: 成功将复杂操作任务（如块重定位）的成功率从25%提升至95%，验证了人机协作下的混合奖励机制能有效加速RL策略在真实世界的收敛与泛化。Successfully increases the success rate of complex manipulation tasks (such as block repositioning) from 25% to 95%, validating that the hybrid reward mechanism under human-machine collaboration can effectively accelerate the convergence and generalization of RL policies in the real world.

方法: Method: 提出ORPA框架，在冻结的ACT主干网络外挂载轻量级残差策略模块，根据人类实时反馈动态调整关节空间动作，而非重新训练整个模型。Proposing the ORPA framework, which attaches a lightweight residual policy module to a frozen ACT backbone network to dynamically adjust joint-space actions based on real-time human feedback, rather than retraining the entire model.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需全量重训的即时策略适配，显著降低了人机协作中的试错成本，但揭示了依赖高质量人类反馈及处理反馈噪声仍是当前工程落地的关键瓶颈。Achieving instant policy adaptation without full-scale retraining, significantly reducing trial-and-error costs in human-robot collaboration, while revealing that reliance on high-quality human feedback and handling feedback noise remain key engineering bottlenecks for deployment.

Autonomously Acquiring Robot Manipulation Skills with Language-Driven Quality-Diversity
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决灵巧操作任务中依赖专家设计质量多样性（QD）指标导致的泛化性差与人力成本高的问题，试图通过人机协作的自动化机制降低人类干预门槛。Aiming to solve the issues of poor generalization and high human labor costs caused by reliance on expert-designed Quality Diversity (QD) metrics in dexterous manipulation tasks, this work attempts to lower the threshold for human intervention through a human-machine collaborative automation mechanism.
方法: Method: 引入大语言模型（LLM）作为动态“裁判”，将自然语言指令转化为运动原语库与评估指标，驱动QD算法在仿真环境中自动生成多样化的操作策略。Large Language Models (LLMs) are introduced as dynamic 'judges' to convert natural language instructions into motion primitive libraries and evaluation metrics, driving QD algorithms to automatically generate diverse manipulation strategies in simulation environments.
推进: Advance: 实现了从高层语义意图到低层运动原语的自动化映射，摆脱了传统方法对人工设计奖励函数的依赖，为构建无需专家微调的自主技能获取框架提供了新范式。Automated mapping from high-level semantic intent to low-level motion primitives is achieved, breaking away from the traditional dependence on manually designed reward functions, and providing a new paradigm for building autonomous skill acquisition frameworks without expert fine-tuning.

Zeva: In-Context Causal Learning for Generalizable Embodied Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有具身智能系统在真实部署中在线微调算力消耗大、难以适应未见物理特性的痛点，探索利用人类演示数据辅助的上下文因果学习机制。Addressing the pain points of high online fine-tuning computational costs and difficulty adapting to unseen physical characteristics in real-world deployment of existing embodied intelligence systems, this work explores context-aware causal learning mechanisms assisted by human demonstration data.
方法: Method: 构建双时隙记忆模块，将动作与状态变化编码为因果信号，使冻结的VLA基座模型能通过“回忆”历史交互上下文进行推理，而非依赖梯度更新。A dual-timeslot memory module is constructed, encoding actions and state changes as causal signals, enabling frozen VLA foundation models to reason by 'recalling' historical interaction contexts rather than relying on gradient updates.
推进: Advance: 证明了在无需在线训练的情况下，利用因果记忆检索可显著提升策略在重复任务中的鲁棒性与成功率，为VLA模型在资源受限场景下的实时人机协作提供了高效的部署方案。It is demonstrated that causal memory retrieval can significantly improve strategy robustness and success rates in repetitive tasks without online training, providing an efficient deployment solution for VLA models in resource-constrained scenarios for real-time human-robot collaboration.

灵巧手抓取与双臂协同Dexterous hand grasping and dual-arm coordination

本方向聚焦于灵巧手抓取与双臂协同，核心在于解决高自由度下的感知 - 规划 - 控制闭环。演进脉络从早期依赖大规模合成数据（如DexGraspNet 2.0）解决静态杂乱场景，转向利用多模态融合（触觉+视觉）与生成式数字孪生（RoboTwin系列）降低Sim-to-Real鸿沟。近期研究更强调长视界任务分解（FurnitureVLA）、世界模型的细粒度条件化（DexAC-WM）以及真实世界多视角触觉数据的构建（Deform360, RoboTacDex）。尽管在静态抓取上已实现高成功率，但在动态交互、非刚性物体操作及双臂复杂协同的泛化能力上，仍面临物理建模缺失与数据稀缺的严峻挑战，正从“暴力堆料”向“结构化理解与物理一致性”深度演进。This direction focuses on dexterous hand grasping and dual-arm coordination, with the core challenge being the perception-planning-control closed loop under high degrees of freedom. The evolution has shifted from early reliance on large-scale synthetic data (e.g., DexGraspNet 2.0) to solve static cluttered scenarios, towards utilizing multi-modal fusion (touch + vision) and generative digital twins (RoboTwin series) to reduce the Sim-to-Real gap. Recent research emphasizes long-horizon task decomposition (FurnitureVLA), fine-grained conditioning of world models (DexAC-WM), and the construction of real-world multi-view tactile data (Deform360, RoboTacDex). Although high success rates have been achieved in static grasping, significant challenges remain regarding the generalization capabilities for dynamic interaction, non-rigid object manipulation, and complex dual-arm coordination, due to missing physical modeling and data scarcity. The field is deeply evolving from 'brute-force data stacking' to 'structured understanding and physical consistency'.

动机: Motivation: 解决多机器人灵巧手抓取生成中依赖昂贵接触图优化或事后逆运动学求解导致的实时性差与泛化性不足问题。Addressing the poor real-time performance and insufficient generalization caused by reliance on expensive contact graph optimization or post-hoc inverse kinematics solving in multi-robot dexterous hand grasp generation.

Deltoris: Enabling Real-time VLA Inference in Embodied AI via Bit-level Sparsity and Speculative Inference
动机: Motivation: 针对灵巧手与双臂协同中高频控制带来的巨大计算负载，旨在解决边缘设备上实时推理的算力瓶颈。Addressing the massive computational load caused by high-frequency control in dual-arm and dexterous hand collaboration, we aim to solve the computational bottleneck for real-time inference on edge devices.
方法: Method: 提出Deltoris算法，通过比特级稀疏化与投机推理机制，利用动作序列的差分特性减少冗余计算。We propose the Deltoris algorithm, which utilizes the differential characteristics of action sequences to reduce redundant computation through bit-level sparsification and speculative inference mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 实现了34.2倍的推理加速，证明了在保持控制精度的前提下，通过硬件协同设计可显著降低Sim-to-Real部署的延迟。We achieve a 34.2x inference acceleration, proving that hardware-co-design can significantly reduce Sim-to-Real deployment latency while maintaining control precision.

SSC: A Verifiable Structured Representation for Bimanual Manipulation Labelling
动机: Motivation: 为了解决双臂协同任务中长视界操作标注缺乏结构化约束、难以验证逻辑一致性的问题。To address the problem of lack of structured constraints in long-horizon operation annotations for dual-arm collaboration tasks, making it difficult to verify logical consistency.
方法: Method: 构建SSC框架，引入状态转移表示作为结构化中间态，利用多模型集成进行逻辑体检与信息补全。We construct the SSC framework, introducing state transition representations as structured intermediate states and utilizing multi-model ensembles for logical health checks and information completion.
推进: Advance: 在保持自然语言灵活性的同时建立了可验证的标注标准，有效提升了复杂双臂交互数据的清洗质量与逻辑鲁棒性。We establish verifiable annotation standards while maintaining natural language flexibility, effectively improving the cleaning quality and logical robustness of complex dual-arm interaction data.

A Hierarchical Approach to Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Requiring Time Varying Forces
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在涉及接触的高动态力控任务中反应迟钝及模式平均导致的控制失效问题。Addressing the sluggish response of diffusion policies in high-dynamic force control tasks involving contact and the resulting control failure due to mode averaging.
方法: Method: 提出分层模仿学习架构，将高层规划与底层60Hz力控解耦，并在潜在空间进行离散化处理。We propose a hierarchical imitation learning architecture that decouples high-level planning from low-level 60Hz force control and performs discretization in the latent space.
推进: Advance: 成功在电池拆解等动态任务中实现了高频力反馈控制，解决了传统扩散策略在接触瞬间“想得多、做得慢”的痛点。We successfully achieve high-frequency force feedback control in dynamic tasks such as battery disassembly, solving the pain point of traditional diffusion policies "thinking too much and acting too slowly" at the moment of contact.

Design Optimization for Large High-Force Soft Robot Manipulators Under Gravitational Loads
动机: Motivation: 针对高自由度软体机械手在重力负载下难以兼顾大尺寸与高输出力的设计难题，传统仿真迭代效率低下且难以解析最优几何边界。Addresses the design challenge where high-degree-of-freedom soft robotic hands struggle to balance large size and high output force under gravity loads, where traditional simulation iteration is inefficient and difficult to resolve optimal geometric boundaries.
方法: Method: 提出基于闭式解的数学优化框架，将非线性几何优化问题转化为凸集边界上的单调函数求解，直接推导出不弯折且推力最大的解析尺寸。Proposes a mathematical optimization framework based on closed-form solutions, transforming nonlinear geometric optimization problems into monotonic function solving on convex set boundaries, directly deriving analytical dimensions that are non-bending and maximize thrust.
推进: Advance: 突破了依赖昂贵有限元分析（FEA）迭代的传统设计范式，为软体机器人提供了可解释的理论尺寸上限，但揭示了理论模型对制造工艺精度的苛刻依赖。Breaks through the traditional design paradigm relying on expensive Finite Element Analysis (FEA) iterations, providing interpretable theoretical size upper bounds for soft robots, but revealing the model's stringent dependence on manufacturing process precision.

Throwing a Tight Spiral American Football by a Humanoid Robot
动机: Motivation: 解决人形机器人在动态非刚性物体（如橄榄球）操作中，释放瞬间复杂动力学难以通过传统静态抓取策略精确控制的问题。Addresses the problem where complex dynamics at the moment of release in human-like robots manipulating dynamic non-rigid objects (e.g., rugby balls) are difficult to control precisely through traditional static grasping strategies.
方法: Method: 构建两阶段控制策略将投掷动作拆解为加速与跟随（follow-through）阶段，并定义“紧密螺旋”量化指标，结合离线MPC查找表进行运动规划。Constructs a two-stage control strategy to decompose throwing actions into acceleration and follow-through stages, defining a 'tight spiral' quantitative metric and combining offline MPC lookup tables for motion planning.
推进: Advance: 验证了动态投掷中“跟随动作”对维持物体旋转稳定性的关键作用，填补了高动态交互任务中细粒度物理控制策略的空白，但受限于离线计算与特定硬件泛化性。Validates the critical role of 'follow-through' actions in maintaining object rotational stability during dynamic throwing, filling the gap in fine-grained physical control strategies for high-dynamic interaction tasks, but limited by offline computation and specific hardware generalization.

方法: Method: 提出“臂感知引导”机制，将机械臂约束转化为闭式梯度，在扩散模型的去噪推理阶段直接注入，无需重新训练手-centric 模型即可实现臂手协同。Proposing an "arm-aware guidance" mechanism that transforms arm constraints into closed-form gradients, directly injecting them during the denoising inference stage of diffusion models to achieve arm-hand synergy without retraining hand-centric models.
推进: Advance: 实现了抓取质量与可行性的解耦优化，在不牺牲模型泛化性的前提下显著提升了复杂场景下的任务成功率，标志着灵巧操作从单一手部优化向全链路协同规划的演进。Achieving decoupled optimization of grasp quality and feasibility, significantly improving task success rates in complex scenarios without compromising model generalization, marking the evolution of dexterous manipulation from single-hand optimization to full-link collaborative planning.

Iterative Grasp Pose Refinement: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for 2D Vision
动机: Motivation: 针对传统几何抓取算法在复杂场景下初始候选位姿易失效的问题，旨在利用深度强化学习构建局部优化机制以挽救失败抓取。Addressing the problem where traditional geometric grasping algorithms fail to generate valid initial candidate poses in complex scenarios, aiming to build a local optimization mechanism using deep reinforcement learning to salvage failed grasps.
方法: Method: 提出基于DQN的迭代式抓取位姿精修框架，将2D顶视视觉状态与离散动作空间结合，对初始抓取位姿进行多步修正。Proposing an iterative grasp pose refinement framework based on DQN, combining 2D top-view visual states with a discrete action space to perform multi-step corrections on initial grasp poses.
推进: Advance: 实现了在仿真与真实环境中的100%抓取成功率，验证了RL作为几何算法“补丁”在特定静态场景下的有效性，但受限于离散动作空间与2D视角的泛化能力。Achieving 100% grasp success rates in both simulation and real environments, validating the effectiveness of RL as a 'patch' for geometric algorithms in specific static scenarios, though limited by the generalization capabilities of discrete action spaces and 2D perspectives.

方法: Method: 提出基于接触拓扑条件生成的框架，将物体特征投影至机械手规范空间以解耦语义与几何，实现无需物体标注数据的对象无关训练。Proposing a framework based on contact topology conditional generation, projecting object features into a robotic hand canonical space to decouple semantics and geometry, achieving object-agnostic training without object annotation data.
推进: Advance: 在避免模式坍塌和提升拓扑合规性方面显著优于Dexonomy，证明了在无需特定物体数据下学习多样化抓取策略的可行性，尽管牺牲了部分物理鲁棒性。Significantly outperforming Dexonomy in avoiding mode collapse and improving topology compliance, demonstrating the feasibility of learning diverse grasping strategies without specific object data, although sacrificing some physical robustness.

GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation

方法: Method: 构建生成式物体无关规划器，在推理阶段结合简化的质心力封闭估计与优化策略，将物体约束隐式编码于先验知识中。Constructing a generative object-agnostic planner that combines simplified center-of-mass force closure estimation and optimization strategies during the inference phase, implicitly encoding object constraints into prior knowledge.
推进: Advance: 实现了86.93%的高成功率，验证了将训练重心从“物体数据卷”转向“夹爪几何学习”在特定场景下的有效性，为数据稀缺场景提供了高效替代方案。Achieving a high success rate of 86.93%, validating the effectiveness of shifting the training focus from 'object data volume' to 'gripper geometry learning' in specific scenarios, providing an efficient alternative for data-scarce scenarios.

Learning to infer and manipulate through distributed whole-arm interaction in a soft robot
动机: Motivation: 针对传统灵巧手抓取过度依赖视觉感知且难以处理非刚性形变的问题，探索如何利用软体机器人全身形变作为环境信息的编码源，实现无需视觉的触觉驱动操作。Addressing the problem that traditional dexterous hand grasping overly relies on visual perception and struggles with non-rigid deformation, this work explores utilizing soft robot whole-body deformation as an encoding source for environmental information to achieve vision-free tactile-driven manipulation.
方法: Method: 提出一种分布式全臂交互学习框架，将软体结构的物理形变直接映射为控制策略，利用本体触觉反馈替代外部视觉输入来推断物体状态并执行抓取。A distributed full-arm interaction learning framework is proposed, directly mapping the physical deformation of soft structures to control policies, utilizing proprioceptive tactile feedback to replace external visual input for inferring object states and executing grasps.
推进: Advance: 证明了软体形变本身具备编码环境几何与物理特性的能力，将物理交互噪声转化为有效情报，为高自由度非刚性物体操作提供了新的感知 - 控制范式。It is proven that soft deformation itself possesses the capability to encode environmental geometry and physical properties, transforming physical interaction noise into effective intelligence, providing a new perception-control paradigm for high-degree-of-freedom non-rigid object manipulation.

T3S: Improving Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Task-Specific Feature Selector and Scheduler
动机: Motivation: 解决双臂协同与多任务场景下，不同任务间特征冲突导致的策略干扰问题，试图在保持多任务学习效率的同时提升对特定灵巧操作任务的适应性。Addressing the strategy interference caused by feature conflicts between different tasks in dual-arm collaboration and multi-task scenarios, this work attempts to improve adaptability to specific dexterous manipulation tasks while maintaining multi-task learning efficiency.
方法: Method: 设计T3S框架，结合超网络与课程学习机制，通过任务特定特征选择器动态生成掩码，以细粒度地隔离和选择共享网络中的有效特征。The T3S framework is designed, combining hypernetworks and curriculum learning mechanisms to dynamically generate masks via task-specific feature selectors, finely isolating and selecting effective features within the shared network.
推进: Advance: 在仿真环境中显著提升了多任务强化学习的收敛速度与性能，为复杂双臂协同任务中的特征解耦提供了高效的工程化基线，尽管尚未验证真实物理交互。Significant improvements in convergence speed and performance of multi-task reinforcement learning are achieved in simulation environments, providing an efficient engineering baseline for feature disentanglement in complex dual-arm collaboration tasks, although real physical interaction has not yet been verified.

VLA 大模型架构与空间智能VLA Large Model Architecture and Spatial Intelligence

在VLA大模型与空间智能视角下，灵巧操作领域正从单纯的“感知 - 动作”映射向具备深层空间推理、物理约束感知及长程记忆的系统演进。早期工作如RoboBrain和Magma试图统一多模态架构，但受限于2D视觉的空间缺失。随后，Spatial Forcing和MolmoAct通过引入隐式3D对齐或显式空间推理流水线，显著提升了模型对几何关系的理解。近期研究进一步融合强化学习（VLA-RL, Z-1）与反思机制（PhysReflect-VLA），并引入事件驱动记忆（EventVLA）与测试时扩展（E-TTS）来解决长程任务中的非马尔可夫性与实时性矛盾。整体演进逻辑是从“盲目执行”转向“深思熟虑且物理可行”的具身智能，强调空间先验、物理约束与动态规划能力的深度融合。From the perspective of VLA large models and spatial intelligence, the field of dexterous manipulation is evolving from simple 'perception-action' mapping to systems possessing deep spatial reasoning, physical constraint perception, and long-term memory. Early works like RoboBrain and Magma attempted to unify multi-modal architectures but were limited by the lack of spatial information in 2D vision. Subsequently, Spatial Forcing and MolmoAct significantly improved models' understanding of geometric relationships by introducing implicit 3D alignment or explicit spatial reasoning pipelines. Recent research further integrates reinforcement learning (VLA-RL, Z-1) and reflection mechanisms (PhysReflect-VLA), while introducing event-driven memory (EventVLA) and test-time scaling (E-TTS) to resolve the contradiction between non-Markovianity and real-time performance in long-horizon tasks. The overall evolutionary logic shifts from 'blind execution' to 'thoughtful and physically feasible' embodied intelligence, emphasizing the deep fusion of spatial priors, physical constraints, and dynamic planning capabilities.

论文
动机: Motivation: 传统VLA模型依赖庞大的像素级预测和过度推理，导致计算冗余，难以在边缘设备实现高效的实时空间交互。Traditional VLA models rely on massive pixel-level predictions and over-reasoning, causing computational redundancy and making efficient real-time spatial interaction difficult to achieve on edge devices.
方法: Method: 提出SLIM架构，将动作与观测的交互直接学习在紧凑的潜在空间，摒弃像素解码器，仅用0.5B参数建模物理交互。We propose the SLIM architecture, which directly learns the interaction between actions and observations in a compact latent space, discarding pixel decoders and modeling physical interactions with only 0.5B parameters.
推进: Advance: 证明了紧凑的潜在交互表征足以替代大模型，在保持高性能的同时实现了推理速度提升6倍和显存减半。We demonstrate that compact latent interaction representations are sufficient to replace large models, achieving a 6x inference speedup and halved memory usage while maintaining high performance.

论文
动机: Motivation: 试图将长程记忆硬编码进神经网络权重会导致非马尔可夫任务处理能力下降，且难以应对动态变化的环境状态。Attempting to hardcode long-term memory into neural network weights leads to degraded performance on non-Markovian tasks and makes it difficult to cope with dynamically changing environmental states.
方法: Method: 提出HyMeS混合学习框架，将技能习得保留在VLA权重中，而将状态记忆与逻辑规划显式交由代码变量管理。We propose the HyMeS hybrid learning framework, retaining skill acquisition in VLA weights while explicitly delegating state memory and logical planning to code variables for management.
推进: Advance: 通过“技能在权重、记忆在代码”的解耦策略，有效解决了VLA在处理长程任务时的“脸盲”与非马尔可夫性问题。Through the decoupling strategy of "skills in weights, memory in code," we effectively solve the "face blindness" and non-Markovian issues of VLA models when handling long-horizon tasks.

论文
动机: Motivation: 现有VLA缓存机制仅依赖视觉像素变化，无法感知模型在关键空间对齐任务中的认知不确定性，导致“盲目”缓存引发决策幻觉。Existing VLA caching mechanisms rely solely on visual pixel changes, failing to perceive the model's cognitive uncertainty in critical spatial alignment tasks, leading to "blind" caching that induces decision hallucinations.
方法: Method: 提出神经内省门控机制，利用logits margin作为裁判，当模型对动作预测置信度不足时动态禁用缓存并重新推理。We propose a neural introspection gating mechanism that utilizes logits margin as a referee, dynamically disabling caching and triggering re-inference when the model's confidence in action prediction is insufficient.

动机: Motivation: 现有动作分词器过度追求轨迹重建的像素级精度，抹杀了“推”、“放”等动作背后的语义差异，导致空间推理与物理意图脱节。Existing action tokenizers overly pursue pixel-level accuracy in trajectory reconstruction, erasing semantic differences behind actions like "push" and "place," leading to a disconnect between spatial reasoning and physical intent.

论文
动机: Motivation: 解决现有VLA架构（如VLM-as-encoder）将推理与动作割裂的问题，试图在单一自回归流中重建模型深层的空间思考与物理规划能力。Addresses the problem of current VLA architectures (such as VLM-as-encoder) separating reasoning from action, attempting to reconstruct the model's deep spatial thinking and physical planning capabilities within a single autoregressive flow.
方法: Method: 构建G0.5架构，采用纯自回归流同时处理推理与动作生成，将“思考”过程直接嵌入动作Token序列，而非依赖外部编码器或扩散头。Constructs the G0.5 architecture, adopting a pure autoregressive flow to simultaneously process reasoning and action generation, directly embedding the 'thinking' process into the action token sequence rather than relying on external encoders or diffusion heads.
推进: Advance: 在DROID基准上实现了显著的零样本迁移性能提升，证明了将空间推理内化为动作生成流的一部分能显著增强模型的长程规划与物理可行性，但同时也暴露了其在低对比度视觉场景下的空间感知短板。Achieves significant zero-shot transfer performance improvements on the DROID benchmark, proving that internalizing spatial reasoning as part of the action generation flow significantly enhances the model's long-horizon planning and physical feasibility, while also exposing its spatial perception shortcomings in low-contrast visual scenarios.

论文
动机: Motivation: 揭示当前VLA模型在空间感知上的脆弱性，指出其看似通用的策略实则缺乏对物理世界纹理与几何关系的深层鲁棒理解，易受表面视觉干扰。Reveals the fragility of current VLA models in spatial perception, indicating that their seemingly universal strategies lack deep robust understanding of physical world textures and geometric relationships, making them susceptible to superficial visual interference.
方法: Method: 提出跨任务通用对抗纹理攻击，通过简单的表面纹理贴附，在无需微调的情况下诱导OpenVLA和π0.5在多个灵巧操作任务中产生灾难性的动作偏移。Proposes a cross-task universal adversarial texture attack that, through simple surface texture attachment, induces catastrophic action shifts in OpenVLA and π0.5 across multiple dexterous manipulation tasks without fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 证明了现有VLA架构在空间先验与物理约束感知上的巨大漏洞，表明模型可能仅依赖浅层视觉特征而非深层空间推理，挑战了“通用策略”的可靠性。Demonstrates significant vulnerabilities in current VLA architectures regarding spatial priors and physical constraint perception, suggesting models may rely on shallow visual features rather than deep spatial reasoning, challenging the reliability of 'universal strategies'.

动机: Motivation: 为消除传统思维链（CoT）中语言生成带来的幻觉风险及推理延迟，探索VLA模型如何更高效地利用空间语言信息。To eliminate the hallucination risks and reasoning latency caused by language generation in traditional Chain-of-Thought (CoT), exploring how VLA models can more efficiently utilize spatial language information.
方法: Method: 提出In-Context VLA架构，通过外部工具将空间证据转化为文本注入上下文，强制模型仅进行“读题”而非“写作”。Proposing an In-Context VLA architecture that converts spatial evidence into text via external tools and injects it into the context, forcing the model to only 'read the problem' rather than 'write'.
推进: Advance: 将推理延迟降低4.6倍并显著提升SOTA性能，证实了VLA的核心在于理解空间语言而非生成语言，实现了推理与动作的彻底解耦。Reducing reasoning latency by 4.6 times and significantly improving SOTA performance, confirming that the core of VLA lies in understanding spatial language rather than generating language, achieving a complete decoupling of reasoning and action.

推进: Advance: 在LIBERO-PRO基准上实现了68%的物理实验成功率，证明了在空间扰动下，显式行为树比端到端轨迹记忆更具鲁棒性。Achieved a 68% physical experiment success rate on the LIBERO-PRO benchmark, proving that explicit behavior trees are more robust than end-to-end trajectory memory under spatial perturbations.

Track4Action: Distilling World-Centric 3D Tracker into Vision-Language-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 为了解决VLA模型缺乏真实物理世界动态感知能力，以及直接部署大型世界模型导致推理延迟过高的矛盾。To address the contradiction between the lack of real-world dynamic perception capabilities in VLA models and the excessively high inference latency caused by directly deploying large world models.
方法: Method: 采用知识蒸馏策略，利用冻结的3D世界跟踪器（Track4World）作为教师，将真实演示中的物理证据蒸馏至轻量级VLA学生模型中。We adopt a knowledge distillation strategy, using a frozen 3D world tracker (Track4World) as a teacher to distill physical evidence from real demonstrations into a lightweight VLA student model.
推进: Advance: 成功在零部署负担下提升了模型对3D世界变化的理解能力，证明了利用离线预训练的物理先验可以显著增强在线灵巧操作的空间推理精度。We successfully enhance the model's understanding of 3D world changes under zero deployment burden, proving that leveraging offline pre-trained physical priors can significantly improve spatial reasoning accuracy for online dexterous manipulation.

LiLa-WAM: Lightweight Latent Reasoning World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对当前VLA模型过度追求语义理解而忽视物理控制精度，导致模型臃肿且难以在资源受限设备上部署的问题。Addressing the problem where current VLA models overly pursue semantic understanding while neglecting physical control precision, resulting in bloated models that are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained devices.
方法: Method: 提出轻量级潜在推理世界动作模型，利用冻结的视觉骨干网络提取特征，专注于未来状态预测与动作生成的联合训练，摒弃复杂的语言生成。Propose a lightweight latent reasoning world action model that leverages a frozen visual backbone network to extract features, focusing on joint training for future state prediction and action generation while discarding complex language generation.
推进: Advance: 证明了在机器人控制中，精简的视觉 - 动作联合推理比庞大的语义理解更能提升空间操作的准确性与实时性，为资源受限场景提供了高效的空间智能方案。Demonstrate that in robotic control, streamlined visual-action joint reasoning enhances spatial operation accuracy and real-time performance more effectively than extensive semantic understanding, providing an efficient spatial intelligence solution for resource-constrained scenarios.
2026-08
Continue or Replan? Bernoulli-Continuation Policy Learning for Adaptive Horizon Execution
动机: Motivation: 为了解决固定步长执行策略在长程任务中缺乏灵活性，导致模型在失败时无法及时触发重规划而陷入无效循环的问题。Address the issue where fixed-step execution strategies lack flexibility in long-horizon tasks, causing the model to fail to trigger replanning in time upon failure and fall into ineffective loops.
方法: Method: 将连续决策建模为伯努利过程，使模型能够自适应地判断是继续执行当前动作块还是立即触发重规划。Model continuous decision-making as a Bernoulli process, enabling the model to adaptively determine whether to continue executing the current action block or immediately trigger replanning.
推进: Advance: 通过动态调整执行 horizon 显著提升了低成功率任务的表现，证明了在空间智能中，“何时思考”（重规划时机）比单纯的“思考什么”（动作预测）对任务完成度更为关键。Significantly improve performance on low-success-rate tasks by dynamically adjusting the execution horizon, proving that in spatial intelligence, 'when to think' (replanning timing) is more critical for task completion than simply 'what to think' (action prediction).

DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA模型在跨机器人本体泛化时难以适应不同动力学特性的痛点，旨在解耦通用物理规律与特定控制策略。Addressing the pain point where existing VLA models struggle to adapt to different dynamic characteristics during cross-robot embodiment generalization, aiming to decouple general physical laws from specific control policies.
方法: Method: 提出共享动力学先验与本体特定控制解耦架构，通过未来预测头学习物理规律，动作头适配具体形态。Proposing a shared dynamic prior and embodiment-specific control decoupling architecture, learning physical laws via a future prediction head and adapting to specific morphologies via an action head.
推进: Advance: 实现了模型对跨形态机器人（如人形与单臂）的通用物理理解，证明了将“物理规律”与“执行控制”分离能显著提升泛化能力。Achieving general physical understanding of the model across morphologies (e.g., humanoid and single-arm), proving that separating 'physical laws' from 'execution control' can significantly enhance generalization capabilities.

推进: Advance: 成功将长程任务成功率从26.9%提升至57.7%，证明了显式化任务分解与交接机制比单纯优化端到端策略更有效。Successfully increasing long-horizon task success rates from 26.9% to 57.7%, demonstrating that explicit task decomposition and handoff mechanisms are more effective than simply optimizing end-to-end policies.

Temporal Forcing: 4D Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型缺乏时间维度导致在遮挡或长程任务中空间状态感知失效的问题，旨在通过引入时间一致性约束来增强模型对动态环境的深层理解。Addressing the issue where existing VLA models lack a temporal dimension, leading to failure in spatial state perception during occlusion or long-horizon tasks, this work aims to enhance the model's deep understanding of dynamic environments by introducing temporal consistency constraints.
方法: Method: 提出 Temporal Forcing 机制，利用预训练的 4D 生成模型（StreamVGGT）作为教师，在训练阶段强制对齐 VLA 的历史状态与当前帧的 4D 几何表示，推理时仅保留轻量级历史通道。A Temporal Forcing mechanism is proposed, utilizing a pre-trained 4D generative model (StreamVGGT) as a teacher to force alignment between the VLA's historical states and the 4D geometric representation of the current frame during training, retaining only lightweight historical channels during inference.
推进: Advance: 成功将 4D 时空先验蒸馏至 VLA 架构中，显著提升了模型在 LIBERO 等长程任务中的成功率，证明了时间维度对齐是解决非马尔可夫性空间推理的关键。4D spatiotemporal priors are successfully distilled into the VLA architecture, significantly improving success rates in long-horizon tasks such as LIBERO, demonstrating that temporal dimension alignment is key to solving non-Markovian spatial reasoning.
2026-08
PAVE: Predictive Alignment and Value-Guided Evolution for World-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 为了解决行为克隆策略中“来者不拒”导致的次优执行问题，以及在线推理时计算资源与实时决策之间的矛盾，试图解耦世界预测与动作执行。To address the suboptimal execution caused by 'accepting all comers' in behavior cloning policies and the contradiction between computational resources and real-time decision-making during online inference, this work attempts to decouple world prediction from action execution.
方法: Method: 构建 PAVE 框架，采用 JEPA 架构在离线阶段进行多轮“预测世界”与“价值评估”的卷生卷死训练，上线时仅保留经过价值筛选的精简执行器，实现训练与推理的解耦。The PAVE framework is constructed, adopting a JEPA architecture for multi-round 'predict world' and 'value evaluation' training in the offline phase, retaining only a pruned executor filtered by value during the online phase to achieve decoupling of training and inference.
推进: Advance: 验证了将未来预测作为训练辅助而非在线推理组件的有效性，在保持高感知能力的同时大幅降低了在线计算开销，为资源受限场景下的高效灵巧操作提供了新范式。The effectiveness of using future prediction as a training auxiliary rather than an online inference component is validated, significantly reducing online computational overhead while maintaining high perceptual capabilities, providing a new paradigm for efficient dexterous manipulation in resource-constrained scenarios.

CometVLA: Co-Training on an Embodied Data Pyramid towards Physical Understanding
动机: Motivation: 针对 VLA 模型缺乏物理常识导致在真实物理交互中泛化能力差（如光照、纹理变化下失效）的痛点，试图建立视觉语言模型与具身动作之间的物理语义桥梁。Addressing the pain point that VLA models lack physical common sense, leading to poor generalization in real physical interactions (e.g., failure under lighting or texture changes), this work attempts to establish a physical semantic bridge between vision-language models and embodied actions.
方法: Method: 提出具身数据金字塔与 CometData 数据集，设计 GAP Token 作为信息瓶颈隔离语言与动作头，通过共训练策略将 VQA 任务中的物理理解直接迁移至机器人动作域。An embodied data pyramid and the CometData dataset are proposed, designing GAP Tokens as an information bottleneck to isolate language and action heads, and directly transferring physical understanding from VQA tasks to the robot action domain through co-training strategies.
推进: Advance: 系统性证明了 VLM 的物理常识可转化为具身动作策略，填补了纯视觉 VQA 与机器人物理交互之间的语义鸿沟，尽管在极端真实场景下仍有提升空间。It is systematically proven that VLM physical common sense can be converted into embodied action strategies, filling the semantic gap between pure visual VQA and robot physical interaction, although there is still room for improvement in extreme real-world scenarios.

扩散策略与 3D 表示融合Fusion of Diffusion Policies and 3D Representations

该方向致力于解决扩散策略在复杂操作任务中缺乏3D几何感知与物理一致性的痛点。核心思路是将3D表示（点云、高斯球、几何先验）深度融入扩散/流匹配策略的观测编码与动作生成过程，从单纯的像素预测转向显式的3D动态建模。演进路径从早期的利用3D点作为动作锚点（如Point Policy），发展到构建包含物理约束的3D世界模型（如PhysMani, 3D Point WM），再到通过频域优化和几何先验蒸馏提升推理效率与鲁棒性。这一脉络旨在消除2D视觉的歧义性，赋予机器人理解物体空间结构、遮挡关系及物理交互本质的能力，从而实现更精准的灵巧操作。This direction aims to address the pain points of diffusion policies lacking 3D geometric perception and physical consistency in complex manipulation tasks. The core idea is to deeply integrate 3D representations (point clouds, Gaussian spheres, geometric priors) into the observation encoding and action generation processes of diffusion/flow matching policies, shifting from simple pixel prediction to explicit 3D dynamic modeling. The evolutionary path has progressed from early methods using 3D points as action anchors (e.g., Point Policy), to constructing 3D world models with physical constraints (e.g., PhysMani, 3D Point WM), and further to improving inference efficiency and robustness through frequency domain optimization and geometric prior distillation. This lineage aims to eliminate the ambiguity of 2D vision, endowing robots with the ability to understand object spatial structures, occlusion relationships, and the essence of physical interactions, thereby achieving more precise dexterous manipulation.

—
论文
动机: Motivation: 克服传统方法依赖CAD模型或无法在操作过程中实时理解物体细粒度语义结构的局限，解决“感知即抓取”的最后一公里问题。We overcome the limitations of traditional methods relying on CAD models or being unable to understand fine-grained semantic structures of objects in real-time during manipulation, solving the last-mile problem of "perception is grasping."
方法: Method: 构建在线部件级语义重建系统，利用VLM进行语义定位、SAM3分割及TSDF建图，并通过投票机制将语义信息融入扩散策略的观测编码。We construct an online part-level semantic reconstruction system that utilizes VLM for semantic localization, SAM3 for segmentation, and TSDF for mapping, integrating semantic information into the observation encoding of diffusion policies via a voting mechanism.
推进: Advance: 实现了不依赖先验CAD模型的实时语义重建，显著提升了机器人在细薄部件操作中的几何理解能力与任务成功率。We achieve real-time semantic reconstruction without relying on prior CAD models, significantly enhancing the robot's geometric understanding and task success rates in manipulating thin and delicate components.

CAER: Causal Action Effect Reweighting for World Model Training
动机: Motivation: 克服世界模型训练中动作干预信号被背景噪声淹没的难题，确保策略学习聚焦于物体真实的物理因果效应而非虚假相关性。Overcoming the challenge of action intervention signals being drowned out by background noise in world model training, ensuring strategy learning focuses on the true physical causal effects of objects rather than spurious correlations.
方法: Method: 设计因果动作效应重加权（CAER）算法，通过对比有无动作干预下的状态差异，自适应地将梯度权重分配给受动作显著影响的3D区域。Designing the Causal Action Effect Reweighting (CAER) algorithm, which adaptively assigns gradient weights to 3D regions significantly affected by actions by comparing state differences with and without action interventions.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需外部深度估计或光流输入的在线物理一致性优化，显著提升了模型对物体遮挡关系及接触物理本质的理解能力，增强了扩散策略在复杂操作中的鲁棒性。Achieving online physical consistency optimization without external depth estimation or optical flow inputs, significantly enhancing the model's understanding of object occlusion relationships and the essence of contact physics, thereby improving the robustness of diffusion policies in complex manipulation tasks.

具身智能评测与基准Embodied AI Evaluation and Benchmarks

具身灵巧操作评测正从依赖黑盒逆动力学（IDM）的闭环测试，转向基于显式运动学接地与中间表示的透明化评估体系。核心思路在于解耦感知、规划与执行误差，通过构建细粒度中间表示（如RoboInter1.5）统一仿真与真实数据，并利用无IDM基准（如KineBench）精准诊断物理幻觉。演进路径显示，评测不再仅关注任务成功率，更强调对复杂物理交互的可解释性分析，以及利用LLM简化环境以测试规划器在杂乱场景下的鲁棒性，从而推动世界模型从“像素生成”向“物理理解”跨越。Embodied dexterous manipulation evaluation is shifting from closed-loop testing relying on black-box inverse dynamics (IDM) to a transparent evaluation system based on explicit kinematic grounding and intermediate representations. The core idea lies in decoupling perception, planning, and execution errors, unifying simulation and real-world data by constructing fine-grained intermediate representations (such as RoboInter1.5), and precisely diagnosing physical hallucinations using non-IDM benchmarks (such as KineBench). The evolutionary path shows that evaluation no longer focuses solely on task success rates but emphasizes interpretability analysis of complex physical interactions and the use of LLMs to simplify environments for testing planner robustness in cluttered scenarios, thereby driving world models to transcend from "pixel generation" to "physical understanding."

论文
动机: Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型评测过度关注像素级视觉逼真度，缺乏对物理交互一致性的显式诊断，难以识别模型在复杂操作中的物理幻觉。Existing action-conditioned world model evaluations overly focus on pixel-level visual fidelity, lacking explicit diagnosis of physical interaction consistency, making it difficult to identify physical hallucinations in complex operations.
方法: Method: 构建WorldSimProbe诊断框架，通过“动作-运动-交互”链条的细粒度约束，量化仿真器在物理动态与运动学接地上的保真度偏差。We construct the WorldSimProbe diagnostic framework, quantifying fidelity deviations in simulators regarding physical dynamics and kinematic grounding through fine-grained constraints on the "action-motion-interaction" chain.
推进: Advance: 首次将评测重心从视觉相似性转向物理因果一致性，能够精准定位模型是在“模拟”视觉还是“理解”物理，推动了灵巧操作基准向透明化评估体系演进。For the first time, we shift the evaluation focus from visual similarity to physical causal consistency, enabling precise localization of whether a model is "simulating" vision or "understanding" physics, driving the evolution of dexterous manipulation benchmarks toward transparent evaluation systems.

论文
动机: Motivation: 传统灵巧操作评测依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注或难以解耦的进度信号，导致奖励模型难以在复杂物理交互中精准区分任务质量与执行效率。Traditional dexterous manipulation evaluation relies on expensive human preference annotations or difficult-to-decouple progress signals, making it hard for reward models to precisely distinguish task quality and execution efficiency in complex physical interactions.
方法: Method: 提出基于“时间距离”的无监督价值学习框架，利用7000小时机器人数据直接预测完成剩余任务所需的时间作为价值信号，替代传统偏好标注。We propose an unsupervised value learning framework based on "time distance," utilizing 7,000 hours of robot data to directly predict the time required to complete the remaining task as a value signal, replacing traditional preference annotations.
推进: Advance: 证明了时间距离可作为稳健的价值代理，在无需人工干预的情况下实现了优于SOTA偏好监督模型的奖励预测，为解耦规划与执行误差提供了新的可解释性基准。We prove that time distance can serve as a robust value proxy, achieving reward prediction superior to SOTA preference-supervised models without human intervention, providing a new interpretable benchmark for decoupling planning and execution errors.

论文
动机: Motivation: 指出聚合成功率指标掩盖了 VLA 模型在双臂操作中存在的特定初始构型偏差（姿势依赖症），导致对模型真实能力的误判。Points out that aggregated success rates mask specific initial configuration biases (posture dependency syndrome) in VLA models for dual-arm manipulation, leading to misjudgment of the model's true capabilities.
方法: Method: 提出 HandPriorScore 量化指标，通过关节级干预精准定位导致策略失效的因果手柄（如 Rarm,3 和 Rarm,5），解耦感知与执行误差。Proposes the HandPriorScore quantification metric, using joint-level intervention to precisely locate causal handles causing policy failure (e.g., Rarm,3 and Rarm,5), decoupling perception and execution errors.
推进: Advance: 首次将“策略诱导的手部先验”定义为可量化的评测维度，揭示了现有基准在状态编码和初始构型敏感性分析上的盲点。For the first time defines 'Policy-Induced Hand Prior' as a quantifiable evaluation dimension, revealing blind spots in current benchmarks regarding state encoding and initial configuration sensitivity analysis.

论文
动机: Motivation: 针对具身智能领域严重的 Sim2Real 鸿沟，批判单纯依赖真实数据采集或纯仿真训练无法验证真实世界的灵巧操作能力。Addresses the severe Sim2Real gap in the field of embodied intelligence, critiquing that relying solely on real data collection or pure simulation training cannot verify dexterous manipulation capabilities in the real world.
方法: Method: 发起 RoboSynChallenge，采用合成数据主导训练与真实世界严格评估相结合的混合范式，强制模型在真实噪声环境中泛化。Initiates RoboSynChallenge, adopting a hybrid paradigm combining synthetic-data-dominated training with strict real-world evaluation, forcing models to generalize in real noisy environments.
推进: Advance: 验证了合成数据在提升真实灵巧性上的潜力，同时揭示了当前模型在复杂物理交互中仍高度依赖特定环境分布，推动了评测向“虚实混合”与“物理理解”演进。Validates the potential of synthetic data in enhancing real-world dexterity while revealing that current models still heavily rely on specific environmental distributions in complex physical interactions, driving evaluation towards 'hybrid virtual-real' and 'physical understanding'.

论文
动机: Motivation: 揭示当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在跨任务通用性上的脆弱性，指出黑盒测试无法发现针对物体表面纹理的隐蔽攻击漏洞。Reveals the fragility of current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models regarding cross-task generalization, pointing out that black-box testing fails to detect hidden attack vulnerabilities targeting object surface textures.
方法: Method: 提出跨任务通用对抗纹理（UniTexture），通过单一纹理攻击同时干扰 OpenVLA 和 π0.5 在 LIBERO 基准上的表现，无需针对特定任务调参。Proposes Cross-Task Universal Adversarial Texture (UniTexture), which uses a single texture attack to simultaneously disrupt the performance of OpenVLA and π0.5 on the LIBERO benchmark without task-specific parameter tuning.
推进: Advance: 证明了现有 VLA 模型缺乏对物理交互的深层理解，仅靠像素级特征匹配导致在微小纹理扰动下动作方向完全失控，暴露了评测体系对物理鲁棒性的缺失。Demonstrates that current VLA models lack deep understanding of physical interaction; relying solely on pixel-level feature matching leads to complete loss of action direction under minute texture perturbations, exposing the lack of physical robustness in evaluation systems.

论文
动机: Motivation: 批判现有视频生成基准过度关注视觉保真度，忽视了具身智能核心任务中的物理交互逻辑与动作意图的准确性。Critiques current video generation benchmarks for overemphasizing visual fidelity while neglecting the physical interaction logic and action intent accuracy in core embodied intelligence tasks.
方法: Method: 构建 H2R-Bench，引入跨具身视角，强制评估模型在生成视频中保留人类示范的任务意图与接触证据，而非单纯风格迁移。Constructs H2R-Bench, introducing a cross-embodied perspective to force evaluation of whether generated videos retain human demonstration task intent and contact evidence, rather than mere style transfer.
推进: Advance: 量化了世界模型在视觉逼真度与功能正确性之间的鸿沟，指出“画虎不成反类犬”的现象，推动评测从像素级质量向物理逻辑一致性转变。Quantifies the gap between visual fidelity and functional correctness in world models, pointing out the phenomenon of 'trying to paint a tiger but ending up with a dog', pushing evaluation from pixel-level quality towards physical logic consistency.

动机: Motivation: 针对当前动作条件世界模型在跨机器人实体泛化上表现出的“视觉模式匹配”而非“物理理解”的缺陷，亟需构建能揭示模型物理幻觉的评测基准。Addressing the defect that current action-conditioned world models exhibit 'visual pattern matching' rather than 'physical understanding' in cross-robot entity generalization, necessitating the construction of evaluation benchmarks that reveal model physical hallucinations.

推进: Advance: 实证揭示了单纯堆砌数据无法解决跨实体泛化瓶颈，证明了现有模型缺乏真正的物理动力学理解，并指出了架构创新优于数据规模的演进方向。Empirically revealing that simply piling up data cannot solve the cross-entity generalization bottleneck, proving that existing models lack true physical dynamic understanding, and pointing out the evolutionary direction where architectural innovation surpasses data scale.

论文
动机: Motivation: 为解决JEPA类世界模型中全局分布不坍塌但缺乏物理不变性与反事实动态能力的表征“虚胖”问题，需要显式约束潜在空间以符合物理规律。To address the 'virtual obesity' of representations in JEPA-style world models where global distributions do not collapse but lack physical invariance and counterfactual dynamic capabilities, explicit constraints on the latent space are needed to comply with physical laws.

OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决仿真环境中“任务成功”与“物理安全”脱节的问题，防止机器人通过破坏性手段达成目标。To address the disconnect between 'task success' and 'physical safety' in simulation environments, preventing robots from achieving goals through destructive means.
方法: Method: 构建 OopsieVerse 基准，引入 DamageSim 量化机械、热及流体伤害，将物理损伤信号作为通用安全奖励纳入评估。Constructing the OopsieVerse benchmark, introducing DamageSim to quantify mechanical, thermal, and fluid damage, and incorporating physical damage signals as a universal safety reward into the evaluation.
推进: Advance: 首次实现了跨平台仿真器的统一伤害评估，成功区分了“暴力成功”与“安全成功”，推动了评测从单纯的任务完成向物理安全性延伸。Achieving unified damage evaluation across simulation platforms for the first time, successfully distinguishing between 'violent success' and 'safe success', and promoting the extension of evaluation from mere task completion to physical safety.

推进: Advance: 量化了模型对无关物体位置变化的敏感度，证明了基于白盒分析的“哪里坏了修哪里”策略比盲目全量重训在工程落地中更具效率。Quantifying the model's sensitivity to position changes of irrelevant objects, proving that the 'fix where it breaks' strategy based on white-box analysis is more efficient in engineering deployment than blind full-scale retraining.
2026-08
VLAGuard: A Framework for Evaluating and Mitigating Physical Attention Hijacking in Vision-Language-Action Robots within Wireless Sensor Networks
动机: Motivation: 为了解决现有防御机制忽略动作生成时视觉注意力被物理补丁劫持的致命安全问题，提升 VLA 模型的抗攻击能力。To address the fatal security issue where existing defense mechanisms ignore the hijacking of visual attention by physical patches during action generation, enhancing the attack resistance of VLA models.
方法: Method: 构建 VLAGuard 框架，提出 APFT 防御机制仅调整视觉编码器以阻断物理攻击，并在大规模真实机器人实验中验证。Constructing the VLAGuard framework, proposing the APFT defense mechanism that adjusts only the visual encoder to block physical attacks, and validating it in large-scale real-robot experiments.
推进: Advance: 揭示了 VLA 模型在物理世界中的“玻璃心”特性，证明了机制层面的注意力防御比黑盒测试更能有效保障复杂环境下的执行安全。Revealing the 'glass heart' characteristic of VLA models in the physical world, proving that mechanism-level attention defense is more effective than black-box testing in ensuring execution safety in complex environments.
2026-08
GUARD: Grounding Uncertainty and Ablation-Based Risk Detection for Diffusion-Based VLAs
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散式VLA动作规划中存在的‘物理幻觉’问题，即模型看似执行动作实则未真正理解环境依赖的黑盒现象，亟需一种能穿透KV缓存内部依赖的透明化评估手段。Address the 'physical hallucination' problem in diffusion-based VLA action planning, where models appear to execute actions without truly understanding environmental dependencies—a black box phenomenon—necessitating a transparent evaluation method capable of penetrating internal KV cache dependencies.
方法: Method: 提出GUARD框架，通过直接探测并分析扩散VLA的KV缓存依赖关系，利用关键Token的消融实验来验证动作生成的‘接地’（Grounding）真实性，而非仅依赖动作轨迹的稳定性。Propose the GUARD framework, which directly probes and analyzes KV cache dependency relationships in diffusion VLA, using ablation experiments on key tokens to verify the authenticity of action generation 'grounding', rather than relying solely on action trajectory stability.
推进: Advance: 相比仅基于Embedding距离的SAFE或FIPER，该方法能更精准地诊断动作是否‘瞎蒙’，在未见任务上显著提升了风险检测的ROC-AUC，推动了评测从黑盒统计向内部机制可解释性的跨越。Compared to SAFE or FIPER based solely on Embedding distance, this method more accurately diagnoses whether actions are 'blind guessing', significantly improving ROC-AUC for risk detection on unseen tasks, promoting evaluation from black-box statistics to internal mechanism interpretability.
2026-08
SAFECAST: Robust Failure Detection for VLA Policies with Contrast-Set Training and Calibration
动机: Motivation: 为了解决具身智能在仿真与真实世界迁移时的‘水土不服’及泛化失效问题，需要一种不依赖形式化交换性保证、但能显著提升故障检测鲁棒性的安全评估方案。To address the '水土不服' (maladaptation) and generalization failure of embodied intelligence during simulation-to-reality transfer, a safety evaluation scheme is needed that does not rely on formal interchangeability guarantees but significantly enhances fault detection robustness.
方法: Method: 构建SAFECAST方法，利用对比集训练（Contrast-Set Training）和校准技术，通过引入额外的扰动数据来训练故障检测器，使其能更敏锐地识别VLA策略在复杂场景下的异常行为。Construct the SAFECAST method, utilizing contrast-set training and calibration techniques, introducing additional perturbation data to train the fault detector, enabling it to more sensitively identify anomalous behaviors of VLA policies in complex scenarios.
推进: Advance: 在仿真和真机环境中均实现了F1和ROC-AUC的提升，证明了通过‘用数据换安全’的校准确能比盲目信任模型更早预警物理交互中的潜在风险，弥补了现有基准在安全关键场景下的不足。Achieve improvements in F1 and ROC-AUC in both simulation and real-world environments, proving that calibration via 'trading data for safety' can warn of potential risks in physical interactions earlier than blind trust in models,弥补ing the deficiencies of existing benchmarks in safety-critical scenarios.
2026-08
DRIFT: Derailing Denoising Trajectories of Flow-Matching VLAs with Adversarial Patch Attack
动机: Motivation: 针对当前Flow-Matching VLA模型看似抗扰动实则脆弱的现状，揭示现有评测方法忽略了去噪轨迹ODE结构这一关键物理交互特征，导致对模型鲁棒性的误判。Address the current status of Flow-Matching VLA models, which appear robust to perturbations but are actually fragile, revealing that existing evaluation methods overlook the key physical interaction feature of the denoising trajectory ODE structure, leading to misjudgments of model robustness.
方法: Method: 提出DRIFT攻击方法，利用对抗补丁专门针对去噪轨迹的ODE结构进行干扰，发现仅攻击去噪过程的第一步即可导致模型‘抓瞎’，从而暴露了模型对特定物理交互路径的脆弱性。Propose the DRIFT attack method, which uses adversarial patches to specifically interfere with the ODE structure of denoising trajectories, discovering that attacking only the first step of the denoising process can cause the model to 'go blind', thereby exposing the model's fragility to specific physical interaction paths.
推进: Advance: 打破了π0系列模型抗扰动的迷思，指出了现有安全评估在物理交互可解释性上的漏洞，为构建更严谨的、基于物理轨迹动态的评测基准提供了反面实证。Break the myth of the π0 series models' robustness to perturbations, point out the loopholes in existing safety evaluations regarding physical interaction interpretability, and provide negative empirical evidence for building more rigorous evaluation benchmarks based on physical trajectory dynamics.
2026-08
Prism-GRPO: Faster VLA Policy Optimization via Splitting Same-outcome Groups
动机: Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的具身策略优化在早期训练中因全成功或全失败的样本组被丢弃，导致大量样本浪费且难以利用中间状态的细粒度反馈。Existing GRPO-based embodied policy optimization discards sample groups that are entirely successful or entirely failed during early training, leading to significant sample waste and difficulty in leveraging fine-grained feedback from intermediate states.
方法: Method: 提出Prism-GRPO算法，通过轨迹质量将具有相同结果但执行过程不同的轨迹分组，利用细粒度的质量信号为失败轨迹生成有效梯度。We propose the Prism-GRPO algorithm, which groups trajectories with identical outcomes but different execution processes based on trajectory quality, utilizing fine-grained quality signals to generate effective gradients for failed trajectories.
推进: Advance: 打破了传统评测仅依赖最终任务成功率的局限，实现了对执行过程中接触与运动质量的透明化评估，提升了策略在复杂物理交互中的鲁棒性。This breaks the limitations of traditional evaluation relying solely on final task success rates, achieving transparent assessment of contact and motion quality during execution, thereby enhancing policy robustness in complex physical interactions.
2026-08
Repetition as Reinforcement: Enhancing Sample Efficiency via Instant Episode Repetition in Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 灵巧操作评测中常因样本效率低下而难以区分感知规划误差与执行物理误差，导致对物理幻觉的诊断不精准。In dexterous manipulation evaluation, low sample efficiency often makes it difficult to distinguish between perception planning errors and execution physical errors, leading to imprecise diagnosis of physical hallucinations.
方法: Method: 提出即时 episode 重复（IER）机制，一旦检测到高回报轨迹便立即在环境中重复执行以强化特定动作序列，替代被动的经验回放。We propose the Immediate Episode Repetition (IER) mechanism, which immediately repeats high-reward trajectories in the environment upon detection to reinforce specific action sequences, replacing passive experience replay.
推进: Advance: 通过主动重复高价值交互数据，构建了更清晰的物理反馈闭环，有效解耦了规划与执行误差，为无IDM基准下的物理理解提供了更高效的验证路径。By actively repeating high-value interaction data, we construct a clearer physical feedback loop, effectively decoupling planning and execution errors, and providing a more efficient verification path for physical understanding without an IDM baseline.
2026-08
What Matters for Latent Actions in Robot Learning
动机: Motivation: 针对具身灵巧操作评测中Latent Action Models (LAM) 领域设计复杂化且缺乏系统性验证的现状，旨在通过大规模消融实验厘清各组件对性能的真实贡献。Addressing the current status of complex design and lack of systematic verification in the Latent Action Models (LAM) field within embodied dexterous operation evaluation, aiming to clarify the true contribution of each component through large-scale ablation studies.
方法: Method: 构建了包含41种设计组合的暴力测试基准，系统评估了不同正则化方法与架构变体在仿真环境中的表现，而非依赖单一复杂模型。Constructing a brute-force testing benchmark containing 41 design combinations, systematically evaluating the performance of different regularization methods and architectural variants in simulation environments, rather than relying on a single complex model.
推进: Advance: 揭示了简单自编码器或原始LAPO往往优于过度正则化的复杂模型，推翻了“越复杂越好”的潜规则，为构建透明、可解释的评测体系提供了去伪存真的实证依据。Revealing that simple autoencoders or raw LAPO often outperform over-regularized complex models, overturning the implicit rule that 'the more complex, the better', and providing empirical evidence to remove falsehoods and retain truth for building transparent and interpretable evaluation systems.
2026-08
Behavior-Skill: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Policies in Long-Horizon Tasks
动机: Motivation: 现有任务级基准（如 BEHAVIOR-1K）仅关注宏观成功率，掩盖了长 horizon 任务中复杂的中间交互失败与物理幻觉问题，无法精准定位黑盒模型在细粒度技能执行上的缺陷。Existing task-level benchmarks (e.g., BEHAVIOR-1K) focus solely on macroscopic success rates, masking complex intermediate interaction failures and physical hallucinations in long-horizon tasks, and failing to precisely locate defects in fine-grained skill execution of black-box models.
方法: Method: 提出 Behavior-Skill 基准，将长 horizon 任务强制拆解为细粒度的原子技能单元，构建包含 23 万实例的大规模仿真数据集以评估 VLA 策略的底层能力。Proposing the Behavior-Skill benchmark, which decomposes long-horizon tasks into fine-grained atomic skill units, constructing a large-scale simulation dataset of 230,000 instances to evaluate the underlying capabilities of VLA policies.
推进: Advance: 揭示了主流模型（如 π0.5）在细粒度技能层面的成功率不足 50%，证明单纯增加数据量无法解决复杂物理交互难题，为诊断规划与序列依赖缺失提供了可解释的侧窗。Revealing that mainstream models (e.g., π0.5) achieve less than 50% success rates at the fine-grained skill level, proving that simply increasing data volume cannot solve complex physical interaction challenges, and providing an interpretable window for diagnosing missing planning and sequence dependencies.
